    <section class="content-section">

        <h2>
            Why convert HEIC to JPG?
        </h2>

        <p>
            HEIC and HEIF are commonly used for efficient photo
            storage, particularly in Apple device workflows.
            JPG is a widely recognized format that can be easier
            to use with older applications, websites and workflows.
        </p>

        <p>
            Converting a HEIC photo to JPG can therefore be useful
            when an application or website does not accept the
            original HEIC format.
        </p>

        <p>
            Conversion may change image compression and metadata.
            The converted JPG should be treated as a new image file,
            not an exact container-level copy of the original.
        </p>

    </section>

            <details>

                </p>

            <details>

                <summary>
                    Can I convert iPhone photos from HEIC to JPG?

                </p>

            </details>

            <details>

                <summary>
                    Is the original metadata preserved?
                </summary>

                <p>
                    Metadata preservation is not guaranteed by
                    the client-side conversion library. The output
                    should be treated as a converted JPG image.
                </p>

    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const qualityControl =
        document.getElementById("qualityControl");

    const quality =
        document.getElementById("quality");

    const qualityValue =
        document.getElementById("qualityValue");

    const convertButton =
        document.getElementById("convertButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    const result =
        document.getElementById("result");

    const originalSize =
        document.getElementById("originalSize");

    const convertedSize =
        document.getElementById("convertedSize");

    const savedPercent =
        document.getElementById("savedPercent");

    const downloadButton =
        document.getElementById("downloadButton");

    let selectedFile = null;
    let convertedBlob = null;
    let convertedDownloadUrl = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {

            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function cleanupDownloadUrl() {

        if (convertedDownloadUrl) {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
                convertedDownloadUrl
            );

            convertedDownloadUrl =
                null;
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {

        selectedFile = null;
        convertedBlob = null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "none";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "none";

        convertButton.style.display =
            "none";

        convertButton.disabled =
            true;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus("");
    }

    function isHeicFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return false;
        }

        return (
            /.(heic|heif)$/i.test(
                file.name
            ) ||
            file.type === "image/heic" ||
            file.type === "image/heif"
        );
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        if (!isHeicFile(file)) {

            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a HEIC or HEIF image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "conversion_error",
                {
                    tool:
                        "heic_to_jpg",
                    reason:
                        "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        convertedBlob =
            null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "block";

        convertButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        convertButton.disabled =
            false;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus(
            "HEIC photo ready to convert."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "file_selected",
            {
                tool:
                    "heic_to_jpg",
                file_type:
                    file.type ||
                    "heic"
            }
        );
    }

    quality.addEventListener(
        "input",
        function () {

            qualityValue.textContent =
                this.value + "%";
        }
    );

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFile(
                event.dataTransfer.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    convertButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        async function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select a HEIC image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            if (
                typeof heic2any !==
                "function"
            ) {

                setStatus(
                    "The HEIC converter could not be loaded. Please refresh the page and try again.",
                    "error"
                );

                gtag(
                    "event",
                    "conversion_error",
                    {
                        tool:
                            "heic_to_jpg",
                        reason:
                            "decoder_not_loaded"
                    }
                );

                return;
            }

            convertButton.disabled =
                true;

            downloadButton.style.display =
                "none";

            result.style.display =
                "none";

            setStatus(
                "Converting HEIC to JPG..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "conversion_started",
                {
                    tool:
                        "heic_to_jpg",
                    quality:
                        Number(
                            quality.value
                        )
                }
            );

            try {

                const output =
                    await heic2any({
                        blob:
                            selectedFile,

                        toType:
                            "image/jpeg",

                        quality:
                            Number(
                                quality.value
                            ) / 100
                    });

                /*
                 * heic2any can return a Blob or
                 * an array when multiple=true.
                 *
                 * This tool processes one still image.
                 */
                const blob =
                    Array.isArray(
                        output
                    )
                        ? output[0]
                        : output;

                if (
                    !(blob instanceof Blob)
                ) {

                    throw new Error(
                        "Invalid conversion result."
                    );
                }

                convertedBlob =
                    blob;

                cleanupDownloadUrl();

                convertedDownloadUrl =
                    URL.createObjectURL(
                        blob
                    );

                const originalBytes =
                    selectedFile.size;

                const convertedBytes =
                    blob.size;

                const saved =
                    originalBytes > 0
                        ? Math.max(
                            0,
                            (
                                (
                                    originalBytes -
                                    convertedBytes
                                ) /
                                originalBytes
                            ) * 100
                        )
                        : 0;

                originalSize.textContent =
                    formatFileSize(
                        originalBytes
                    );

                convertedSize.textContent =
                    formatFileSize(
                        convertedBytes
                    );

                savedPercent.textContent =
                    saved.toFixed(
                        1
                    ) + "%";

                result.style.display =
                    "block";

                downloadButton.style.display =
                    "inline-flex";

                setStatus(
                    "Conversion complete. JPG download is ready.",
                    "success"
                );

                gtag(
                    "event",
                    "conversion_completed",
                    {
                        tool:
                            "heic_to_jpg",
                        output_type:
                            "jpg",
                        quality:
                            Number(
                                quality.value
                            ),
                        original_bytes:
                            originalBytes,
                        output_bytes:
                            convertedBytes,
                        saved_percent:
                            Number(
                                saved.toFixed(
                                    1
                                )
                            )
                    }
                );

            } catch (error) {

                console.error(error);

                setStatus(
                    "Could not convert this HEIC file. The file may be unsupported or damaged.",
                    "error"
                );

                gtag(
                    "event",
                    "conversion_error",
                    {
                        tool:
                            "heic_to_jpg",
                        reason:
                            "heic_conversion_failed"
                    }
                );

            } finally {

                convertButton.disabled =
                    false;
            }
        }
    );

    downloadButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !convertedBlob ||
                !convertedDownloadUrl
            ) {
                return;
            }

            const outputName =
                selectedFile.name
                    .replace(
                        /\.(heic|heif)$/i,
                        ""
                    ) +
                ".jpg";

            const link =
                document.createElement(
                    "a"
                );

            link.href =
                convertedDownloadUrl;

            link.download =
                outputName;

            link.style.display =
                "none";

            document.body.appendChild(
                link
            );

            link.click();

            document.body.removeChild(
                link
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "download",
                {
                    tool:
                        "heic_to_jpg",
                    file_type:
                        "jpg"
                }
            );
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupDownloadUrl();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

                step="1"
                aria-label="Compression quality"
            >

            <p class="quality-help">
                Lower values usually create smaller files.
            </p>

        </div>

        <button
            class="button"

            disabled
        >

        <div

        >

            <div class="result-grid">

<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const qualityControl =
        document.getElementById("qualityControl");

    const quality =
        document.getElementById("quality");

    const qualityValue =
        document.getElementById("qualityValue");

    const compressButton =
        document.getElementById("compressButton");

    const downloadButton =
        document.getElementById("downloadButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    const result =
        document.getElementById("result");

    const originalSize =
        document.getElementById("originalSize");

    const compressedSize =
        document.getElementById("compressedSize");

    const savedPercent =
        document.getElementById("savedPercent");

    let selectedFile = null;
    let compressedBlob = null;
    let compressedDownloadUrl = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function cleanupDownloadUrl() {

        if (compressedDownloadUrl) {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
                compressedDownloadUrl
            );

            compressedDownloadUrl =
                null;
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {

        selectedFile = null;
        compressedBlob = null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "none";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "none";

        compressButton.style.display =
            "none";

        compressButton.disabled =
            true;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus("");
    }

    function isSupportedImage(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return false;
        }

        return (
            file.type === "image/jpeg" ||
            file.type === "image/png" ||
            file.type === "image/webp" ||
            /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i.test(
                file.name
            )
        );
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        if (!isSupportedImage(file)) {

            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a JPG, PNG or WebP image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "compression_error",
                {
                    tool: "image_compressor",
                    reason: "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        compressedBlob =
            null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "block";

        compressButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        compressButton.disabled =
            false;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus(
            "Image ready to compress."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "file_selected",
            {
                tool: "image_compressor",
                file_type: file.type || "unknown"
            }
        );
    }

    quality.addEventListener(
        "input",
        function () {

            qualityValue.textContent =
                this.value + "%";
        }
    );

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            const file =
                event.dataTransfer.files[0];

            handleFile(file);
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    compressButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select an image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            compressButton.disabled =
                true;

            downloadButton.style.display =
                "none";

            setStatus(
                "Compressing..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "compression_started",
                {
                    tool: "image_compressor",
                    quality: Number(
                        quality.value
                    ),
                    input_type:
                        selectedFile.type
                }
            );

            const image =
                new Image();

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        if (
                            image.naturalWidth === 0 ||
                            image.naturalHeight === 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid image dimensions."
                            );
                        }

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            image.naturalWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            image.naturalHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        /*
                         * JPEG does not support transparency.
                         * Use a white background when the
                         * input has an alpha channel.
                         */
                        context.fillStyle =
                            "#ffffff";

                        context.fillRect(
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        const inputType =
                            selectedFile.type;

                        let outputType =
                            "image/jpeg";

                        let extension =
                            ".jpg";

                        /*
                         * PNG/WebP are exported as WebP.
                         * JPG/JPEG are exported as JPEG.
                         *
                         * This keeps JPEG images as JPEG and
                         * gives PNG/WebP a browser-friendly
                         * lossy compression path.
                         */
                        if (
                            inputType === "image/jpeg" ||
                            /\.(jpg|jpeg)$/i.test(
                                selectedFile.name
                            )
                        ) {

                            outputType =
                                "image/jpeg";

                            extension =
                                ".jpg";

                        } else {

                            outputType =
                                "image/webp";

                            extension =
                                ".webp";
                        }

                        const compressionQuality =
                            Number(
                                quality.value
                            ) / 100;

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                    sourceUrl
                                );

                                if (!blob) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Could not create the compressed image.",
                                        "error"
                                    );

                                    gtag(
                                        "event",
                                        "compression_error",
                                        {
                                            tool: "image_compressor",
                                            reason: "blob_creation_failed"
                                        }
                                    );

                                    compressButton.disabled =
                                        false;

                                    return;
                                }

                                compressedBlob =
                                    blob;

                                cleanupDownloadUrl();

                                compressedDownloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                const originalBytes =
                                    selectedFile.size;

                                const compressedBytes =
                                    blob.size;

                                const saved =
                                    originalBytes > 0
                                        ? Math.max(
                                            0,
                                            (
                                                (
                                                    originalBytes -
                                                    compressedBytes
                                                ) /
                                                originalBytes
                                            ) * 100
                                        )
                                        : 0;

                                originalSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        originalBytes
                                    );

                                compressedSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        compressedBytes
                                    );

                                savedPercent.textContent =
                                    saved.toFixed(1) + "%";

                                result.style.display =
                                    "block";

                                downloadButton.style.display =
                                    "inline-flex";

                                if (
                                    compressedBytes <
                                    originalBytes
                                ) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Compression complete.",
                                        "success"
                                    );

                                } else {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "The compressed file is not smaller than the original. Try a lower quality setting.",
                                        ""
                                    );
                                }

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "compression_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool: "image_compressor",
                                        input_type:
                                            inputType,
                                        output_type:
                                            outputType,
                                        quality:
                                            Number(
                                                quality.value
                                            ),
                                        original_bytes:
                                            originalBytes,
                                        compressed_bytes:
                                            compressedBytes,
                                        saved_percent:
                                            Number(
                                                saved.toFixed(
                                                    1
                                                )
                                            )
                                    }
                                );

                                compressButton.disabled =
                                    false;

                                /*
                                 * Keep the extension variable
                                 * available to the download handler.
                                 */
                                downloadButton.dataset.extension =
                                    extension;

                                downloadButton.dataset.outputType =
                                    outputType;
                            },
                            outputType,
                            compressionQuality
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            sourceUrl
                        );

                        console.error(error);

                        setStatus(
                            "Compression failed. Please try another image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "compression_error",
                            {
                                tool: "image_compressor",
                                reason: "compression_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        compressButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "compression_error",
                        {
                            tool: "image_compressor",
                            reason: "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    compressButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );

    downloadButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !compressedBlob ||
                !selectedFile ||
                !compressedDownloadUrl
            ) {
                return;
            }

            const extension =
                this.dataset.extension ||
                ".jpg";

            const outputName =
                selectedFile.name
                    .replace(
                        /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i,
                        ""
                    ) + extension;

            const link =
                document.createElement(
                    "a"
                );

            link.href =
                compressedDownloadUrl;

            link.download =
                outputName;

            link.style.display =
                "none";

            document.body.appendChild(
                link
            );

            link.click();

            document.body.removeChild(
                link
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "download",
                {
                    tool: "image_compressor",
                    file_type:
                        this.dataset.outputType ||
                        "image"
                }
            );
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupDownloadUrl();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

        .drop-zone {

        .drop-zone:focus-visible {

<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const fileDimensions =
        document.getElementById("fileDimensions");

    const controls =
        document.getElementById("controls");

    const aspectRatio =
        document.getElementById("aspectRatio");

    const cropArea =
        document.getElementById("cropArea");

    const cropWrapper =
        document.getElementById("cropWrapper");

    const sourceImage =
        document.getElementById("sourceImage");

    const cropBox =
        document.getElementById("cropBox");

    const cropButton =
        document.getElementById("cropButton");

    const downloadButton =
        document.getElementById("downloadButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    const result =
        document.getElementById("result");

    const originalResult =
        document.getElementById("originalResult");

    const croppedResult =
        document.getElementById("croppedResult");

    let selectedFile = null;

    let originalWidth = 0;
    let originalHeight = 0;

    let cropX = 0;
    let cropY = 0;
    let cropWidth = 0;
    let cropHeight = 0;

    let displayScale = 1;

    let dragging = false;
    let dragOffsetX = 0;
    let dragOffsetY = 0;

    let croppedBlob = null;
    let croppedDownloadUrl = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function cleanupDownloadUrl() {

        if (croppedDownloadUrl) {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
                croppedDownloadUrl
            );

            croppedDownloadUrl =
                null;
        }
    }

    function resetCrop() {

        cropX = 0;
        cropY = 0;
        cropWidth = 0;
        cropHeight = 0;

        cropBox.style.display =
            "none";

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        cropButton.disabled =
            false;

        croppedBlob = null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();
    }

    function isSupportedImage(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return false;
        }

        return (
            file.type === "image/jpeg" ||
            file.type === "image/png" ||
            file.type === "image/webp" ||
            /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i.test(
                file.name
            )
        );
    }

    function getDisplaySize() {

        const naturalWidth =
            sourceImage.naturalWidth;

        const naturalHeight =
            sourceImage.naturalHeight;

        const maxWidth =
            Math.min(
                cropWrapper.clientWidth,
                naturalWidth
            );

        const maxHeight =
            560;

        const scale =
            Math.min(
                maxWidth / naturalWidth,
                maxHeight / naturalHeight,
                1
            );

        return {
            width:
                Math.round(
                    naturalWidth * scale
                ),
            height:
                Math.round(
                    naturalHeight * scale
                ),
            scale
        };
    }

    function initializeCropBox() {

        const display =
            getDisplaySize();

        displayScale =
            display.scale;

        sourceImage.style.width =
            display.width + "px";

        sourceImage.style.height =
            display.height + "px";

        cropWrapper.style.width =
            display.width + "px";

        cropWrapper.style.height =
            display.height + "px";

        const ratioValue =
            aspectRatio.value;

        let width =
            display.width * 0.75;

        let height =
            display.height * 0.75;

        if (ratioValue !== "free") {

            const parts =
                ratioValue.split(":");

            const ratio =
                Number(parts[0]) /
                Number(parts[1]);

            if (
                width / height >
                ratio
            ) {
                width =
                    height * ratio;
            } else {
                height =
                    width / ratio;
            }
        }

        cropWidth =
            Math.max(
                30,
                Math.round(width)
            );

        cropHeight =
            Math.max(
                30,
                Math.round(height)
            );

        cropX =
            Math.round(
                (
                    display.width -
                    cropWidth
                ) / 2
            );

        cropY =
            Math.round(
                (
                    display.height -
                    cropHeight
                ) / 2
            );

        updateCropBox();
    }

    function updateCropBox() {

        cropBox.style.display =
            "block";

        cropBox.style.width =
            cropWidth + "px";

        cropBox.style.height =
            cropHeight + "px";

        cropBox.style.left =
            cropX + "px";

        cropBox.style.top =
            cropY + "px";
    }

    function keepCropInside() {

        if (
            cropX < 0
        ) {
            cropX = 0;
        }

        if (
            cropY < 0
        ) {
            cropY = 0;
        }

        if (
            cropX +
            cropWidth >
            sourceImage.clientWidth
        ) {
            cropX =
                sourceImage.clientWidth -
                cropWidth;
        }

        if (
            cropY +
            cropHeight >
            sourceImage.clientHeight
        ) {
            cropY =
                sourceImage.clientHeight -
                cropHeight;
        }
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        if (!isSupportedImage(file)) {

            setStatus(
                "Please select a JPG, PNG or WebP image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "crop_error",
                {
                    tool: "image_cropper",
                    reason: "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        resetCrop();

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        setStatus(
            "Loading image..."
        );

        const sourceUrl =
            URL.createObjectURL(
                file
            );

        sourceImage.onload =
            function () {

                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                    sourceUrl
                );

                originalWidth =
                    sourceImage.naturalWidth;

                originalHeight =
                    sourceImage.naturalHeight;

                fileDimensions.textContent =
                    "Original dimensions: " +
                    originalWidth +
                    " Ã— " +
                    originalHeight +
                    " px";

                controls.style.display =
                    "block";

                cropArea.style.display =
                    "block";

                cropButton.style.display =
                    "inline-flex";

                cropButton.disabled =
                    false;

                initializeCropBox();

                setStatus(
                    "Adjust the crop area and continue."
                );

                gtag(
                    "event",
                    "file_selected",
                    {
                        tool: "image_cropper",
                        file_type:
                            file.type || "unknown"
                    }
                );
            };

        sourceImage.onerror =
            function () {

                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                    sourceUrl
                );

                setStatus(
                    "Unable to read the image.",
                    "error"
                );

                gtag(
                    "event",
                    "crop_error",
                    {
                        tool: "image_cropper",
                        reason:
                            "image_load_failed"
                    }
                );
            };

        sourceImage.src =
            sourceUrl;
    }

    aspectRatio.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            if (
                !sourceImage.naturalWidth
            ) {
                return;
            }

            initializeCropBox();

            gtag(
                "event",
                "crop_ratio_selected",
                {
                    tool: "image_cropper",
                    ratio:
                        this.value
                }
            );
        }
    );

    cropBox.addEventListener(
        "pointerdown",
        function (event) {

            dragging =
                true;

            cropBox.setPointerCapture(
                event.pointerId
            );

            const rect =
                cropBox.getBoundingClientRect();

            dragOffsetX =
                event.clientX -
                rect.left;

            dragOffsetY =
                event.clientY -
                rect.top;

            cropBox.style.cursor =
                "grabbing";
        }
    );

    cropBox.addEventListener(
        "pointermove",
        function (event) {

            if (!dragging) {
                return;
            }

            const wrapperRect =
                cropWrapper.getBoundingClientRect();

            cropX =
                event.clientX -
                wrapperRect.left -
                dragOffsetX;

            cropY =
                event.clientY -
                wrapperRect.top -
                dragOffsetY;

            keepCropInside();

            updateCropBox();
        }
    );

    function stopDragging(event) {

        if (!dragging) {
            return;
        }

        dragging =
            false;

        try {
            cropBox.releasePointerCapture(
                event.pointerId
            );
        } catch (error) {
            // Pointer capture may already be released.
        }

        cropBox.style.cursor =
            "move";
    }

    cropBox.addEventListener(
        "pointerup",
        stopDragging
    );

    cropBox.addEventListener(
        "pointercancel",
        stopDragging
    );

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFile(
                event.dataTransfer.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    cropButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !selectedFile ||
                !sourceImage.naturalWidth
            ) {
                setStatus(
                    "Please select an image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            if (
                cropWidth < 1 ||
                cropHeight < 1
            ) {
                setStatus(
                    "Please select a valid crop area.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            cropButton.disabled =
                true;

            setStatus(
                "Cropping..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "crop_started",
                {
                    tool: "image_cropper",
                    ratio:
                        aspectRatio.value
                }
            );

            const scale =
                sourceImage.naturalWidth /
                sourceImage.clientWidth;

            const sourceX =
                Math.round(
                    cropX * scale
                );

            const sourceY =
                Math.round(
                    cropY * scale
                );

            const sourceCropWidth =
                Math.round(
                    cropWidth * scale
                );

            const sourceCropHeight =
                Math.round(
                    cropHeight * scale
                );

            const canvas =
                document.createElement(
                    "canvas"
                );

            canvas.width =
                sourceCropWidth;

            canvas.height =
                sourceCropHeight;

            const context =
                canvas.getContext(
                    "2d"
                );

            if (!context) {

                setStatus(
                    "Canvas is not supported.",
                    "error"
                );

                cropButton.disabled =
                    false;

                return;
            }

            context.imageSmoothingEnabled =
                true;

            context.imageSmoothingQuality =
                "high";

            context.drawImage(
                sourceImage,
                sourceX,
                sourceY,
                sourceCropWidth,
                sourceCropHeight,
                0,
                0,
                sourceCropWidth,
                sourceCropHeight
            );

            let outputType =
                "image/jpeg";

            let extension =
                ".jpg";

            if (
                selectedFile.type === "image/png" ||
                /\.png$/i.test(
                    selectedFile.name
                )
            ) {

                outputType =
                    "image/png";

                extension =
                    ".png";

            } else if (
                selectedFile.type === "image/webp" ||
                /\.webp$/i.test(
                    selectedFile.name
                )
            ) {

                outputType =
                    "image/webp";

                extension =
                    ".webp";
            }

            const outputQuality =
                outputType === "image/png"
                    ? undefined
                    : 0.92;

            canvas.toBlob(
                function (blob) {

                    if (!blob) {

                        setStatus(
                            "Could not create the cropped image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        cropButton.disabled =
                            false;

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "crop_error",
                            {
                                tool:
                                    "image_cropper",
                                reason:
                                    "blob_creation_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        return;
                    }

                    croppedBlob =
                        blob;

                    cleanupDownloadUrl();

                    croppedDownloadUrl =
                        URL.createObjectURL(
                            blob
                        );

                    originalResult.textContent =
                        originalWidth +
                        " Ã— " +
                        originalHeight +
                        " px";

                    croppedResult.textContent =
                        sourceCropWidth +
                        " Ã— " +
                        sourceCropHeight +
                        " px";

                    result.style.display =
                        "block";

                    downloadButton.style.display =
                        "inline-flex";

                    downloadButton.dataset.extension =
                        extension;

                    downloadButton.dataset.outputType =
                        outputType;

                    setStatus(
                        "Crop complete. Your image is ready to download.",
                        "success"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "crop_completed",
                        {
                            tool:
                                "image_cropper",
                            input_type:
                                selectedFile.type,
                            output_type:
                                outputType,
                            ratio:
                                aspectRatio.value,
                            output_width:
                                sourceCropWidth,
                            output_height:
                                sourceCropHeight
                        }
                    );

                    cropButton.disabled =
                        false;

                },
                outputType,
                outputQuality
            );
        }
    );

    downloadButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !croppedBlob ||
                !croppedDownloadUrl ||
                !selectedFile
            ) {
                return;
            }

            const extension =
                this.dataset.extension ||
                ".jpg";

            const outputName =
                selectedFile.name
                    .replace(
                        /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i,
                        ""
                    ) +
                "-cropped" +
                extension;

            const link =
                document.createElement(
                    "a"
                );

            link.href =
                croppedDownloadUrl;

            link.download =
                outputName;

            document.body.appendChild(
                link
            );

            link.click();

            document.body.removeChild(
                link
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "download",
                {
                    tool:
                        "image_cropper",
                    file_type:
                        this.dataset.outputType ||
                        "image"
                }
            );
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "resize",
        function () {

            if (
                sourceImage.naturalWidth
            ) {
                initializeCropBox();
            }
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupDownloadUrl();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

        <input
            id="fileInput"
            type="file"
            accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,.jpg,.jpeg,.png,.webp"
            aria-label="Choose an image"
        >

        <label
            class="button choose-button"
            for="fileInput"
        >
            Choose image
        </label>

        <div
            class="file-info"
            id="fileInfo"
            aria-live="polite"
        >

            <div
                class="file-name"
                id="fileName"
            ></div>

            <div
                class="file-size"
                id="fileSize"
            ></div>

            <div
                class="original-dimensions"
                id="originalDimensions"
            ></div>

        </div>

        <div
            class="dimensions"
            id="dimensions"
        >

            <div class="dimensions-grid">

                <div class="field">

                    <label for="width">
                        Width (px)
                    </label>

                    <input
                        id="width"
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        max="10000"
                        inputmode="numeric"
                    >

                </div>

                <div class="field">

                    <label for="height">
                        Height (px)
                    </label>

                    <input
                        id="height"
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        max="10000"
                        inputmode="numeric"
                    >

                </div>

            </div>

            <label class="ratio-option">

                <input
                    id="keepRatio"
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked
                >

                <span>
                    Keep aspect ratio
                </span>

            </label>

            <div class="preset-area">

                <label for="preset">
                    Preset
                </label>

                <select
                    id="preset"
                    class="preset-select"
                >

                    <option value="">
                        Custom dimensions
                    </option>

                    <option value="instagram-square">
                        Instagram Square â€” 1080 Ã— 1080
                    </option>

                    <option value="instagram-portrait">
                        Instagram Portrait â€” 1080 Ã— 1350
                    </option>

                    <option value="instagram-landscape">
                        Instagram Landscape â€” 1080 Ã— 566
                    </option>

                    <option value="youtube-thumbnail">
                        YouTube Thumbnail â€” 1280 Ã— 720
                    </option>

                    <option value="facebook-post">
                        Facebook Post â€” 1200 Ã— 630
                    </option>

                    <option value="x-post">
                        X Post â€” 1600 Ã— 900
                    </option>

                    <option value="linkedin-post">
                        LinkedIn Post â€” 1200 Ã— 627
                    </option>

                </select>

            </div>

            <p class="resize-help">
                Maximum output dimensions: 10,000 Ã— 10,000 pixels.
            </p>

        </div>

        <button
            class="button"
            id="resizeButton"
            type="button"
            disabled
        >
            Resize image
        </button>

        <div
            class="status"
            id="status"
            role="status"
            aria-live="polite"
        ></div>

        <div
            class="result"
            id="result"
            aria-live="polite"
        >

            <div class="result-grid">

                <div class="result-card">

                    <span class="result-label">
                        Original size
                    </span>

                    <span
                        class="result-value"
                        id="originalSize"
                    >
                        â€”
                    </span>

                </div>

                <div class="result-card">

                    <span class="result-label">
                        New dimensions
                    </span>

                    <span
                        class="result-value"
                        id="newDimensions"
                    >
                        â€”
                    </span>

                </div>

            </div>

        </div>

        <button
            class="button download-button"
            id="downloadButton"
            type="button"
        >
            Download resized image
        </button>

        <div class="privacy-note">

            <div
                class="privacy-icon"
                aria-hidden="true"
            >
                âœ“
            </div>

            <div>
                <strong>Your file stays on your device.</strong>
                <br>
                Resizing runs directly in your browser.
                Your image does not need to be uploaded to Kyvorix.
            </div>

        </div>

    </section>

    <section class="content-section">

        <h2>
            How to resize an image

        <p>
            Resize an image to custom dimensions or use one
            of the available presets.
        </p>

        <div class="steps">

            <div class="step">

                <div class="step-number">
                    1
                </div>

                <div>

                    <h3>
                        Choose your image
                    </h3>

                    <p>
                        Select a JPG, PNG or WebP image from
                        your device, or drag it into the tool.
                    </p>

                </div>

            </div>

            <div class="step">

                <div class="step-number">
                    2
                </div>

                <div>

                    <h3>
                        Set the dimensions
                    </h3>

                    <p>
                        Enter the width and height you need
                        or select a preset. Keep aspect ratio
                        enabled to preserve the original proportions.
                    </p>

                </div>

            </div>

            <div class="step">

                <div class="step-number">
                    3
                </div>

                <div>

                    <h3>
                        Resize and download
                    </h3>

                    <p>
                        Click Resize image and download the
                        resized file directly to your device.
                    </p>

                </div>

            </div>

        </div>

    </section>

    <section class="content-section">

<footer>

    <div class="footer-inner">

        <a
            href="../index.html"
