</footer>

<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const originalDimensions =
        document.getElementById(
            "originalDimensions"
        );

    const dimensions =
        document.getElementById("dimensions");

    const widthInput =
        document.getElementById("width");

    const heightInput =
        document.getElementById("height");

    const keepRatio =
        document.getElementById("keepRatio");

    const preset =
        document.getElementById("preset");

    const resizeButton =
        document.getElementById("resizeButton");

    const downloadButton =
        document.getElementById("downloadButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    const result =
        document.getElementById("result");

    const originalSize =
        document.getElementById("originalSize");

    const newDimensions =
        document.getElementById("newDimensions");

    let selectedFile = null;
    let originalWidth = 0;
    let originalHeight = 0;
    let resizedBlob = null;
    let resizedDownloadUrl = null;
    let resizingDimension = null;

    const MAX_DIMENSION =
        10000;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function cleanupDownloadUrl() {

        if (resizedDownloadUrl) {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
                resizedDownloadUrl
            );

            resizedDownloadUrl =
                null;
        }
    }

    function isSupportedImage(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return false;
        }

        return (
            file.type === "image/jpeg" ||
            file.type === "image/png" ||
            file.type === "image/webp" ||
            /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i.test(
                file.name
            )
        );
    }

    function resetConverter() {

        selectedFile = null;
        originalWidth = 0;
        originalHeight = 0;

        resizedBlob = null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "none";

        dimensions.style.display =
            "none";

        resizeButton.style.display =
            "none";

        resizeButton.disabled =
            true;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus("");
    }

    function updateHeightFromWidth() {

        if (!originalWidth || !originalHeight) {
            return;
        }

        const newWidth =
            Number(widthInput.value);

        if (!newWidth) {
            return;
        }

        const calculatedHeight =
            Math.round(
                newWidth *
                (
                    originalHeight /
                    originalWidth
                )
            );

        heightInput.value =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Math.min(
                    MAX_DIMENSION,
                    calculatedHeight
                )
            );
    }

    function updateWidthFromHeight() {

        if (!originalWidth || !originalHeight) {
            return;
        }

        const newHeight =
            Number(heightInput.value);

        if (!newHeight) {
            return;
        }

        const calculatedWidth =
            Math.round(
                newHeight *
                (
                    originalWidth /
                    originalHeight
                )
            );

        widthInput.value =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Math.min(
                    MAX_DIMENSION,
                    calculatedWidth
                )
            );
    }

    function validateDimensions() {

        const width =
            Number(widthInput.value);

        const height =
            Number(heightInput.value);

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(width) ||
            !Number.isFinite(height)
        ) {

            setStatus(
                "Please enter valid dimensions.",
                "error"
            );

            return false;
        }

        if (
            width < 1 ||
            height < 1
        ) {

            setStatus(
                "Width and height must be at least 1 pixel.",
                "error"
            );

            return false;
        }

        if (
            width > MAX_DIMENSION ||
            height > MAX_DIMENSION
        ) {

            setStatus(
                "Maximum output dimensions are 10,000 Ã— 10,000 pixels.",
                "error"
            );

            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        if (!isSupportedImage(file)) {

            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a JPG, PNG or WebP image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "resize_error",
                {
                    tool: "image_resizer",
                    reason: "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        resizedBlob =
            null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        const sourceUrl =
            URL.createObjectURL(
                file
            );

        const image =
            new Image();

        image.onload =
            function () {

                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                    sourceUrl
                );

                originalWidth =
                    image.naturalWidth;

                originalHeight =
                    image.naturalHeight;

                widthInput.value =
                    originalWidth;

                heightInput.value =
                    originalHeight;

                originalDimensions.textContent =
                    "Original dimensions: " +
                    originalWidth +
                    " Ã— " +
                    originalHeight +
                    " px";

                dimensions.style.display =
                    "block";

                resizeButton.style.display =
                    "inline-flex";

                resizeButton.disabled =
                    false;

                result.style.display =
                    "none";

                downloadButton.style.display =
                    "none";

                preset.value =
                    "";

                setStatus(
                    "Image ready to resize."
                );

                gtag(
                    "event",
                    "file_selected",
                    {
                        tool: "image_resizer",
                        file_type:
                            file.type || "unknown"
                    }
                );
            };

        image.onerror =
            function () {

                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                    sourceUrl
                );

                resetConverter();

                setStatus(
                    "Unable to read the image.",
                    "error"
                );

                gtag(
                    "event",
                    "resize_error",
                    {
                        tool: "image_resizer",
                        reason: "image_load_failed"
                    }
                );
            };

        image.src =
            sourceUrl;
    }

    widthInput.addEventListener(
        "input",
        function () {

            resizingDimension =
                "width";

            preset.value =
                "";

            if (keepRatio.checked) {
                updateHeightFromWidth();
            }
        }
    );

    heightInput.addEventListener(
        "input",
        function () {

            resizingDimension =
                "height";

            preset.value =
                "";

            if (keepRatio.checked) {
                updateWidthFromHeight();
            }
        }
    );

    keepRatio.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            if (!this.checked) {
                return;
            }

            if (
                resizingDimension === "height"
            ) {
                updateWidthFromHeight();
            } else {
                updateHeightFromWidth();
            }
        }
    );

    preset.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            const presets = {
                "instagram-square": [
                    1080,
                    1080
                ],
                "instagram-portrait": [
                    1080,
                    1350
                ],
                "instagram-landscape": [
                    1080,
                    566
                ],
                "youtube-thumbnail": [
                    1280,
                    720
                ],
                "facebook-post": [
                    1200,
                    630
                ],
                "x-post": [
                    1600,
                    900
                ],
                "linkedin-post": [
                    1200,
                    627
                ]
            };

            const selectedPreset =
                presets[this.value];

            if (!selectedPreset) {
                return;
            }

            widthInput.value =
                selectedPreset[0];

            heightInput.value =
                selectedPreset[1];

            /*
             * A preset represents exact dimensions,
             * so do not alter them automatically.
             */
            resizingDimension =
                null;

            keepRatio.checked =
                false;
        }
    );

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFile(
                event.dataTransfer.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    resizeButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select an image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            if (!validateDimensions()) {
                return;
            }

            const targetWidth =
                Number(
                    widthInput.value
                );

            const targetHeight =
                Number(
                    heightInput.value
                );

            resizeButton.disabled =
                true;

            downloadButton.style.display =
                "none";

            result.style.display =
                "none";

            setStatus(
                "Resizing..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "resize_started",
                {
                    tool: "image_resizer",
                    original_width:
                        originalWidth,
                    original_height:
                        originalHeight,
                    output_width:
                        targetWidth,
                    output_height:
                        targetHeight
                }
            );

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            const image =
                new Image();

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            targetWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            targetHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        /*
                         * Preserve transparent pixels for PNG/WebP.
                         * For JPG, use a white background.
                         */
                        const inputIsJpeg =
                            selectedFile.type ===
                                "image/jpeg" ||
                            /\.(jpg|jpeg)$/i.test(
                                selectedFile.name
                            );

                        if (inputIsJpeg) {

                            context.fillStyle =
                                "#ffffff";

                            context.fillRect(
                                0,
                                0,
                                targetWidth,
                                targetHeight
                            );
                        }

                        /*
                         * High quality scaling when supported.
                         */
                        context.imageSmoothingEnabled =
                            true;

                        context.imageSmoothingQuality =
                            "high";

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            targetWidth,
                            targetHeight
                        );

                        let outputType =
                            "image/jpeg";

                        let extension =
                            ".jpg";

                        if (
                            selectedFile.type ===
                                "image/png" ||
                            /\.png$/i.test(
                                selectedFile.name
                            )
                        ) {

                            outputType =
                                "image/png";

                            extension =
                                ".png";

                        } else if (
                            selectedFile.type ===
                                "image/webp" ||
                            /\.webp$/i.test(
                                selectedFile.name
                            )
                        ) {

                            outputType =
                                "image/webp";

                            extension =
                                ".webp";
                        }

                        /*
                         * PNG is exported as PNG.
                         * WebP is exported as WebP.
                         * JPG is exported as JPEG.
                         */
                        const outputQuality =
                            outputType ===
                                "image/png"
                                ? undefined
                                : 0.92;

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                    sourceUrl
                                );

                                if (!blob) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Could not create the resized image.",
                                        "error"
                                    );

                                    gtag(
                                        "event",
                                        "resize_error",
                                        {
                                            tool: "image_resizer",
                                            reason:
                                                "blob_creation_failed"
                                        }
                                    );

                                    resizeButton.disabled =
                                        false;

                                    return;
                                }

                                resizedBlob =
                                    blob;

                                cleanupDownloadUrl();

                                resizedDownloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                originalSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        selectedFile.size
                                    );

                                newDimensions.textContent =
                                    targetWidth +
                                    " Ã— " +
                                    targetHeight +
                                    " px";

                                result.style.display =
                                    "block";

                                downloadButton.style.display =
                                    "inline-flex";

                                downloadButton.dataset.extension =
                                    extension;

                                downloadButton.dataset.outputType =
                                    outputType;

                                setStatus(
                                    "Resize complete. Your image is ready to download.",
                                    "success"
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "resize_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool: "image_resizer",
                                        input_type:
                                            selectedFile.type,
                                        output_type:
                                            outputType,
                                        original_width:
                                            originalWidth,
                                        original_height:
                                            originalHeight,
                                        output_width:
                                            targetWidth,
                                        output_height:
                                            targetHeight
                                    }
                                );

                                resizeButton.disabled =
                                    false;
                            },
                            outputType,
                            outputQuality
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            sourceUrl
                        );

                        console.error(error);

                        setStatus(
                            "Resize failed. Please try another image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "resize_error",
                            {
                                tool: "image_resizer",
                                reason:
                                    "resize_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        resizeButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "resize_error",
                        {
                            tool: "image_resizer",
                            reason:
                                "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    resizeButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );

    downloadButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !resizedBlob ||
                !selectedFile ||
                !resizedDownloadUrl
            ) {
                return;
            }

            const extension =
                this.dataset.extension ||
                ".jpg";

            const outputName =
                selectedFile.name
                    .replace(
                        /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i,
                        ""
                    ) + extension;

            const link =
                document.createElement(
                    "a"
                );

            link.href =
                resizedDownloadUrl;

            link.download =
                outputName;

            document.body.appendChild(
                link
            );

            link.click();

            document.body.removeChild(
                link
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "download",
                {
                    tool: "image_resizer",
                    file_type:
                        this.dataset.outputType ||
                        "image"
                }
            );
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupDownloadUrl();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

<!--
    Minimal PDF writer.
    This avoids external libraries and keeps processing local.
-->
<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const imageList =
        document.getElementById("imageList");

    const imageItems =
        document.getElementById("imageItems");

    const imageCount =
        document.getElementById("imageCount");

    const settings =
        document.getElementById("settings");

    const pageSize =
        document.getElementById("pageSize");

    const orientation =
        document.getElementById("orientation");

    const createPdfButton =
        document.getElementById("createPdfButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    const result =
        document.getElementById("result");

    const resultPages =
        document.getElementById("resultPages");

    const resultSize =
        document.getElementById("resultSize");

    const downloadButton =
        document.getElementById("downloadButton");

    let selectedFiles = [];
    let generatedPdfBlob = null;
    let generatedPdfUrl = null;

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function cleanupPdfUrl() {

        if (generatedPdfUrl) {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
                generatedPdfUrl
            );

            generatedPdfUrl =
                null;
        }
    }

    function isSupportedImage(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return false;
        }

        return (
            file.type === "image/jpeg" ||
            file.type === "image/png" ||
            file.type === "image/webp" ||
            /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i.test(
                file.name
            )
        );
    }

    function renderFileList() {

        imageItems.innerHTML = "";

        imageCount.textContent =
            selectedFiles.length +
            (
                selectedFiles.length === 1
                    ? " image"
                    : " images"
            );

        if (!selectedFiles.length) {

            imageList.style.display =
                "none";

            settings.style.display =
                "none";

            createPdfButton.style.display =
                "none";

            createPdfButton.disabled =
                true;

            return;
        }

        imageList.style.display =
            "block";

        settings.style.display =
            "block";

        createPdfButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        createPdfButton.disabled =
            false;

        selectedFiles.forEach(
            function (file, index) {

                const item =
                    document.createElement(
                        "div"
                    );

                item.className =
                    "image-item";

                const thumbnail =
                    document.createElement(
                        "img"
                    );

                thumbnail.className =
                    "thumb";

                thumbnail.alt =
                    "";

                const thumbnailUrl =
                    URL.createObjectURL(
                        file
                    );

                thumbnail.src =
                    thumbnailUrl;

                thumbnail.onload =
                    function () {
                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            thumbnailUrl
                        );
                    };

                const info =
                    document.createElement(
                        "div"
                    );

                const name =
                    document.createElement(
                        "div"
                    );

                name.className =
                    "image-item-name";

                name.textContent =
                    (
                        index + 1
                    ) +
                    ". " +
                    file.name;

                const size =
                    document.createElement(
                        "div"
                    );

                size.className =
                    "image-item-size";

                size.textContent =
                    formatFileSize(
                        file.size
                    );

                info.appendChild(
                    name
                );

                info.appendChild(
                    size
                );

                const remove =
                    document.createElement(
                        "button"
                    );

                remove.type =
                    "button";

                remove.className =
                    "remove-button";

                remove.textContent =
                    "Remove";

                remove.setAttribute(
                    "aria-label",
                    "Remove " +
                    file.name
                );

                remove.addEventListener(
                    "click",
                    function () {

                        selectedFiles.splice(
                            index,
                            1
                        );

                        renderFileList();

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "pdf_image_removed",
                            {
                                tool:
                                    "image_to_pdf"
                            }
                        );
                    }
                );

                item.appendChild(
                    thumbnail
                );

                item.appendChild(
                    info
                );

                item.appendChild(
                    remove
                );

                imageItems.appendChild(
                    item
                );
            }
        );
    }

    function handleFiles(fileList) {

        const files =
            Array.from(
                fileList || []
            );

        const validFiles =
            files.filter(
                isSupportedImage
            );

        if (!validFiles.length) {

            setStatus(
                "Please select JPG, PNG or WebP images.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "pdf_error",
                {
                    tool:
                        "image_to_pdf",
                    reason:
                        "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFiles =
            selectedFiles.concat(
                validFiles
            );

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        cleanupPdfUrl();

        renderFileList();

        setStatus(
            selectedFiles.length +
            " image(s) ready for PDF."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "pdf_files_selected",
            {
                tool:
                    "image_to_pdf",
                file_count:
                    validFiles.length
            }
        );
    }

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFiles(
                this.files
            );

            this.value =
                "";
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFiles(
                event.dataTransfer.files
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    async function loadImage(file) {

        return new Promise(
            function (resolve, reject) {

                const image =
                    new Image();

                const url =
                    URL.createObjectURL(
                        file
                    );

                image.onload =
                    function () {

                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            url
                        );

                        resolve(
                            image
                        );
                    };

                image.onerror =
                    function () {

                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            url
                        );

                        reject(
                            new Error(
                                "Unable to read image."
                            )
                        );
                    };

                image.src =
                    url;
            }
        );
    }

    function getPageDimensions(
        imageWidth,
        imageHeight
    ) {

        if (
            pageSize.value ===
            "fit"
        ) {

            return {
                width:
                    imageWidth,
                height:
                    imageHeight
            };
        }

        const sizes = {

            a4: {
                portrait: {
                    width: 595.28,
                    height: 841.89
                },
                landscape: {
                    width: 841.89,
                    height: 595.28
                }
            },

            letter: {
                portrait: {
                    width: 612,
                    height: 792
                },
                landscape: {
                    width: 792,
                    height: 612
                }
            }
        };

        const size =
            sizes[
                pageSize.value
            ];

        if (
            orientation.value ===
            "portrait"
        ) {
            return size.portrait;
        }

        if (
            orientation.value ===
            "landscape"
        ) {
            return size.landscape;
        }

        return imageWidth >= imageHeight
            ? size.landscape
            : size.portrait;
    }

    function uint8ToBinary(
        bytes
    ) {

        let result = "";

        const chunkSize =
            0x8000;

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < bytes.length;
            i += chunkSize
        ) {

            result += String.fromCharCode.apply(
                null,
                bytes.subarray(
                    i,
                    Math.min(
                        i + chunkSize,
                        bytes.length
                    )
                )
            );
        }

        return result;
    }

    async function createPdf() {

        if (!selectedFiles.length) {
            return;
        }

        createPdfButton.disabled =
            true;

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus(
            "Creating PDF..."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "pdf_creation_started",
            {
                tool:
                    "image_to_pdf",
                file_count:
                    selectedFiles.length,
                page_size:
                    pageSize.value,
                orientation:
                    orientation.value
            }
        );

        try {

            const images = [];

            for (
                const file of selectedFiles
            ) {

                const image =
                    await loadImage(
                        file
                    );

                images.push({
                    image,
                    file
                });
            }

            /*
             * Build a simple PDF using JPEG-compressed
             * canvas output. PNG/WebP inputs are rendered
             * to JPEG for compact PDF embedding.
             */
            const objects = [];
            const pageObjectIds = [];
            const contentObjectIds = [];
            const imageObjectIds = [];

            let nextObjectId = 1;

            const catalogId =
                nextObjectId++;

            const pagesId =
                nextObjectId++;

            images.forEach(
                function () {

                    pageObjectIds.push(
                        nextObjectId++
                    );

                    contentObjectIds.push(
                        nextObjectId++
                    );

                    imageObjectIds.push(
                        nextObjectId++
                    );
                }
            );

            function canvasToJpeg(
                image
            ) {

                const canvas =
                    document.createElement(
                        "canvas"
                    );

                canvas.width =
                    image.naturalWidth;

                canvas.height =
                    image.naturalHeight;

                const context =
                    canvas.getContext(
                        "2d"
                    );

                if (!context) {
                    throw new Error(
                        "Canvas is not supported."
                    );
                }

                context.fillStyle =
                    "#ffffff";

                context.fillRect(
                    0,
                    0,
                    canvas.width,
                    canvas.height
                );

                context.drawImage(
                    image,
                    0,
                    0
                );

                return {
                    canvas,
                    dataUrl:
                        canvas.toDataURL(
                            "image/jpeg",
                            0.92
                        )
                };
            }

            const imageDataList =
                images.map(
                    function (entry) {

                        const data =
                            canvasToJpeg(
                                entry.image
                            );

                        const base64 =
                            data.dataUrl
                                .split(
                                    ","
                                )[1];

                        const binary =
                            atob(
                                base64
                            );

                        const bytes =
                            new Uint8Array(
                                binary.length
                            );

                        for (
                            let i = 0;
                            i < binary.length;
                            i++
                        ) {
                            bytes[i] =
                                binary.charCodeAt(
                                    i
                                );
                        }

                        return {
                            width:
                                entry.image
                                    .naturalWidth,
                            height:
                                entry.image
                                    .naturalHeight,
                            bytes
                        };
                    }
                );

            objects[catalogId] =
                "<< /Type /Catalog /Pages " +
                pagesId +
                " 0 R >>";

            objects[pagesId] =
                "<< /Type /Pages /Kids [" +
                pageObjectIds
                    .map(
                        id =>
                            id +
                            " 0 R"
                    )
                    .join(" ") +
                "] /Count " +
                images.length +
                " >>";

            images.forEach(
                function (entry, index) {

                    const pageId =
                        pageObjectIds[
                            index
                        ];

                    const contentId =
                        contentObjectIds[
                            index
                        ];

                    const imageId =
                        imageObjectIds[
                            index
                        ];

                    const imageData =
                        imageDataList[
                            index
                        ];

                    const page =
                        getPageDimensions(
                            imageData.width,
                            imageData.height
                        );

                    const margin =
                        pageSize.value ===
                        "fit"
                            ? 0
                            : 24;

                    const availableWidth =
                        Math.max(
                            1,
                            page.width -
                            margin * 2
                        );

                    const availableHeight =
                        Math.max(
                            1,
                            page.height -
                            margin * 2
                        );

                    const scale =
                        Math.min(
                            availableWidth /
                                imageData.width,
                            availableHeight /
                                imageData.height
                        );

                    const drawWidth =
                        imageData.width *
                        scale;

                    const drawHeight =
                        imageData.height *
                        scale;

                    const drawX =
                        (
                            page.width -
                            drawWidth
                        ) / 2;

                    const drawY =
                        (
                            page.height -
                            drawHeight
                        ) / 2;

                    const content =
                        "q\n" +
                        drawWidth.toFixed(
                            2
                        ) +
                        " 0 0 " +
                        drawHeight.toFixed(
                            2
                        ) +
                        " " +
                        drawX.toFixed(
                            2
                        ) +
                        " " +
                        drawY.toFixed(
                            2
                        ) +
                        " cm\n" +
                        "/Im1 Do\n" +
                        "Q";

                    objects[pageId] =
                        "<< /Type /Page " +
                        "/Parent " +
                        pagesId +
                        " 0 R " +
                        "/MediaBox [0 0 " +
                        page.width.toFixed(
                            2
                        ) +
                        " " +
                        page.height.toFixed(
                            2
                        ) +
                        "] " +
                        "/Resources << /XObject << /Im1 " +
                        imageId +
                        " 0 R >> >> " +
                        "/Contents " +
                        contentId +
                        " 0 R >>";

                    objects[contentId] =
                        "<< /Length " +
                        content.length +
                        " >>\nstream\n" +
                        content +
                        "\nendstream";

                    objects[imageId] =
                        {
                            type:
                                "image",
                            width:
                                imageData.width,
                            height:
                                imageData.height,
                            bytes:
                                imageData.bytes
                        };
                }
            );

            let pdf =
                "%PDF-1.4\n";

            const offsets =
                new Array(
                    nextObjectId
                ).fill(0);

            for (
                let id = 1;
                id < nextObjectId;
                id++
            ) {

                offsets[id] =
                    new TextEncoder()
                        .encode(
                            pdf
                        )
                        .length;

                pdf +=
                    id +
                    " 0 obj\n";

                const object =
                    objects[id];

                if (
                    object &&
                    object.type ===
                    "image"
                ) {

                    pdf +=
                        "<< /Type /XObject " +
                        "/Subtype /Image " +
                        "/Width " +
                        object.width +
                        " " +
                        "/Height " +
                        object.height +
                        " " +
                        "/ColorSpace /DeviceRGB " +
                        "/BitsPerComponent 8 " +
                        "/Filter /DCTDecode " +
                        "/Length " +
                        object.bytes.length +
                        " >>\n" +
                        "stream\n";

                    const prefix =
                        new TextEncoder()
                            .encode(
                                pdf
                            );

                    const binary =
                        uint8ToBinary(
                            object.bytes
                        );

                    pdf = {
                        text:
                            prefix,
                        binary,
                        suffix:
                            "\nendstream\n" +
                            "endobj\n"
                    };

                } else {

                    pdf +=
                        object +
                        "\nendobj\n";
                }
            }

            /*
             * The low-level PDF builder above needs binary-safe
             * assembly, so rebuild using byte arrays.
             */
            const chunks = [];

            const header =
                new TextEncoder()
                    .encode(
                        "%PDF-1.4\n"
                    );

            chunks.push(
                header
            );

            const byteOffsets =
                new Array(
                    nextObjectId
                ).fill(0);

            let totalBytes =
                header.length;

            function pushBytes(
                bytes
            ) {

                chunks.push(
                    bytes
                );

                totalBytes +=
                    bytes.length;
            }

            for (
                let id = 1;
                id < nextObjectId;
                id++
            ) {

                byteOffsets[id] =
                    totalBytes;

                const object =
                    objects[id];

                if (
                    object &&
                    object.type ===
                    "image"
                ) {

                    const head =
                        new TextEncoder()
                            .encode(
                                id +
                                " 0 obj\n" +
                                "<< /Type /XObject " +
                                "/Subtype /Image " +
                                "/Width " +
                                object.width +
                                " " +
                                "/Height " +
                                object.height +
                                " " +
                                "/ColorSpace /DeviceRGB " +
                                "/BitsPerComponent 8 " +
                                "/Filter /DCTDecode " +
                                "/Length " +
                                object.bytes.length +
                                " >>\n" +
                                "stream\n"
                            );

                    pushBytes(
                        head
                    );

                    pushBytes(
                        object.bytes
                    );

                    pushBytes(
                        new TextEncoder()
                            .encode(
                                "\nendstream\n" +
                                "endobj\n"
                            )
                    );

                } else {

                    const text =
                        new TextEncoder()
                            .encode(
                                id +
                                " 0 obj\n" +
                                object +
                                "\nendobj\n"
                            );

                    pushBytes(
                        text
                    );
                }
            }

            const xrefOffset =
                totalBytes;

            let xref =
                "xref\n" +
                "0 " +
                nextObjectId +
                "\n" +
                "0000000000 65535 f \n";

            for (
                let id = 1;
                id < nextObjectId;
                id++
            ) {

                xref +=
                    String(
                        byteOffsets[id]
                    ).padStart(
                        10,
                        "0"
                    ) +
                    " 00000 n \n";
            }

            xref +=
                "trailer\n" +
                "<< /Size " +
                nextObjectId +
                " /Root " +
                catalogId +
                " 0 R >>\n" +
                "startxref\n" +
                xrefOffset +
                "\n%%EOF";

            pushBytes(
                new TextEncoder()
                    .encode(
                        xref
                    )
            );

            const pdfBytes =
                new Uint8Array(
                    totalBytes
                );

            let position = 0;

            chunks.forEach(
                function (chunk) {

                    pdfBytes.set(
                        chunk,
                        position
                    );

                    position +=
                        chunk.length;
                }
            );

            generatedPdfBlob =
                new Blob(
                    [pdfBytes],
                    {
                        type:
                            "application/pdf"
                    }
                );

            cleanupPdfUrl();

            generatedPdfUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    generatedPdfBlob
                );

            resultPages.textContent =
                images.length;

            resultSize.textContent =
                formatFileSize(
                    generatedPdfBlob.size
                );

            result.style.display =
                "block";

            downloadButton.style.display =
                "inline-flex";

            setStatus(
                "PDF created successfully.",
                "success"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "pdf_creation_completed",
                {
                    tool:
                        "image_to_pdf",
                    page_count:
                        images.length,
                    pdf_size:
                        generatedPdfBlob.size,
                    page_size:
                        pageSize.value,
                    orientation:
                        orientation.value
                }
            );

        } catch (error) {

            console.error(error);

            setStatus(
                "Could not create the PDF. Please try again with different images.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "pdf_error",
                {
                    tool:
                        "image_to_pdf",
                    reason:
                        "pdf_creation_failed"
                }
            );
        }

        createPdfButton.disabled =
            false;
    }

    createPdfButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        createPdf
    );

    downloadButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !generatedPdfBlob ||
                !generatedPdfUrl
            ) {
                return;
            }

            const link =
                document.createElement(
                    "a"
                );

            link.href =
                generatedPdfUrl;

            link.download =
                "kyvorix-images.pdf";

            document.body.appendChild(
                link
            );

            link.click();

            document.body.removeChild(
                link
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "download",
                {
                    tool:
                        "image_to_pdf",
                    file_type:
                        "application/pdf"
                }
            );
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupPdfUrl();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const qualityControl =
        document.getElementById("qualityControl");

    const quality =
        document.getElementById("quality");

    const qualityValue =
        document.getElementById("qualityValue");

    const compressButton =
        document.getElementById("compressButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    const result =
        document.getElementById("result");

    const originalSize =
        document.getElementById("originalSize");

    const compressedSize =
        document.getElementById("compressedSize");

    const savedPercent =
        document.getElementById("savedPercent");

    const downloadButton =
        document.getElementById("downloadButton");

    let selectedFile = null;
    let compressedBlob = null;
    let compressedDownloadUrl = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function cleanupDownloadUrl() {

        if (compressedDownloadUrl) {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
                compressedDownloadUrl
            );

            compressedDownloadUrl =
                null;
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {

        selectedFile = null;
        compressedBlob = null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "none";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "none";

        compressButton.style.display =
            "none";

        compressButton.disabled =
            true;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus("");
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        const isJpg =
            file.type === "image/jpeg" ||
            /\.(jpg|jpeg)$/i.test(
                file.name
            );

        if (!isJpg) {

            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a JPG or JPEG image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "compression_error",
                {
                    tool:
                        "jpg_compressor",
                    reason:
                        "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        compressedBlob =
            null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "block";

        compressButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        compressButton.disabled =
            false;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus(
            "JPG ready to compress."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "file_selected",
            {
                tool:
                    "jpg_compressor",
                file_type:
                    "jpg"
            }
        );
    }

    quality.addEventListener(
        "input",
        function () {

            qualityValue.textContent =
                this.value + "%";
        }
    );

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFile(
                event.dataTransfer.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    compressButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select a JPG image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            compressButton.disabled =
                true;

            downloadButton.style.display =
                "none";

            result.style.display =
                "none";

            setStatus(
                "Compressing JPG..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "compression_started",
                {
                    tool:
                        "jpg_compressor",
                    quality:
                        Number(
                            quality.value
                        )
                }
            );

            const image =
                new Image();

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        if (
                            image.naturalWidth === 0 ||
                            image.naturalHeight === 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid image dimensions."
                            );
                        }

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            image.naturalWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            image.naturalHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        /*
                         * JPG does not support transparency.
                         * Painting a white background gives a
                         * predictable result even if the source
                         * image behaves unexpectedly.
                         */
                        context.fillStyle =
                            "#ffffff";

                        context.fillRect(
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        const jpegQuality =
                            Number(
                                quality.value
                            ) / 100;

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                    sourceUrl
                                );

                                if (!blob) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Could not create the compressed JPG.",
                                        "error"
                                    );

                                    gtag(
                                        "event",
                                        "compression_error",
                                        {
                                            tool:
                                                "jpg_compressor",
                                            reason:
                                                "blob_creation_failed"
                                        }
                                    );

                                    compressButton.disabled =
                                        false;

                                    return;
                                }

                                compressedBlob =
                                    blob;

                                cleanupDownloadUrl();

                                compressedDownloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                const originalBytes =
                                    selectedFile.size;

                                const compressedBytes =
                                    blob.size;

                                const saved =
                                    originalBytes > 0
                                        ? Math.max(
                                            0,
                                            (
                                                (
                                                    originalBytes -
                                                    compressedBytes
                                                ) /
                                                originalBytes
                                            ) * 100
                                        )
                                        : 0;

                                originalSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        originalBytes
                                    );

                                compressedSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        compressedBytes
                                    );

                                savedPercent.textContent =
                                    saved.toFixed(
                                        1
                                    ) + "%";

                                result.style.display =
                                    "block";

                                downloadButton.style.display =
                                    "inline-flex";

                                if (
                                    compressedBytes <
                                    originalBytes
                                ) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Compression complete.",
                                        "success"
                                    );

                                } else {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "The result is not smaller than the original. Try a lower quality setting.",
                                        ""
                                    );
                                }

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "compression_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool:
                                            "jpg_compressor",
                                        input_type:
                                            "jpg",
                                        output_type:
                                            "jpg",
                                        quality:
                                            Number(
                                                quality.value
                                            ),
                                        original_bytes:
                                            originalBytes,
                                        compressed_bytes:
                                            compressedBytes,
                                        saved_percent:
                                            Number(
                                                saved.toFixed(
                                                    1
                                                )
                                            )
                                    }
                                );

                                compressButton.disabled =
                                    false;
                            },
                            "image/jpeg",
                            jpegQuality
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            sourceUrl
                        );

                        console.error(error);

                        setStatus(
                            "Compression failed. Please try another JPG image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "compression_error",
                            {
                                tool:
                                    "jpg_compressor",
                                reason:
                                    "compression_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        compressButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the JPG image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "compression_error",
                        {
                            tool:
                                "jpg_compressor",
                            reason:
                                "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    compressButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );

    downloadButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !compressedBlob ||
                !compressedDownloadUrl ||
                !selectedFile
            ) {
                return;
            }

            const outputName =
                selectedFile.name
                    .replace(
                        /\.(jpg|jpeg)$/i,
                        ""
                    ) +
                "-compressed.jpg";

            const link =
                document.createElement(
                    "a"
                );

            link.href =
                compressedDownloadUrl;

            link.download =
                outputName;

            link.style.display =
                "none";

            document.body.appendChild(
                link
            );

            link.click();

            document.body.removeChild(
                link
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "download",
                {
                    tool:
                        "jpg_compressor",
                    file_type:
                        "jpg"
                }
            );
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupDownloadUrl();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>
