<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const convertButton =
        document.getElementById("convertButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    let selectedFile = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {
        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(message, type = "") {
        status.textContent = message;
        status.className = "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(type);
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {
        selectedFile = null;

        fileInfo.style.display = "none";

        convertButton.style.display = "none";
        convertButton.disabled = true;

        setStatus("");
    }

    function handleFile(file) {
        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        const isJpg =
            file.type === "image/jpeg" ||
            /\.(jpg|jpeg)$/i.test(file.name);

        if (!isJpg) {
            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a JPG or JPEG image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag("event", "conversion_error", {
                tool: "jpg_to_png",
                reason: "invalid_file_type"
            });

            return;
        }

        selectedFile = file;

        fileName.textContent = file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(file.size);

        fileInfo.style.display = "block";

        convertButton.style.display = "inline-flex";
        convertButton.disabled = false;

        setStatus(
            "Image ready to convert."
        );

        gtag("event", "file_selected", {
            tool: "jpg_to_png",
            file_type: "jpg"
        });
    }

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {
            handleFile(this.files[0]);
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add("dragover");
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {
            dropZone.classList.remove("dragover");
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove("dragover");

            const file =
                event.dataTransfer.files[0];

            handleFile(file);
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {
            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {
                event.preventDefault();
                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    convertButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {
                setStatus(
                    "Please select a JPG image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            convertButton.disabled = true;

            setStatus(
                "Converting..."
            );

            gtag("event", "conversion_started", {
                tool: "jpg_to_png"
            });

            const image =
                new Image();

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        if (
                            image.naturalWidth === 0 ||
                            image.naturalHeight === 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid image dimensions."
                            );
                        }

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            image.naturalWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            image.naturalHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                if (!blob) {
                                    throw new Error(
                                        "Could not create PNG."
                                    );
                                }

                                const downloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                const link =
                                    document.createElement(
                                        "a"
                                    );

                                const outputName =
                                    selectedFile.name
                                        .replace(
                                            /\.(jpg|jpeg)$/i,
                                            ""
                                        ) + ".png";

                                link.href =
                                    downloadUrl;

                                link.download =
                                    outputName;

                                document.body.appendChild(
                                    link
                                );

                                link.click();

                                document.body.removeChild(
                                    link
                                );

                                setStatus(
                                    "Conversion complete. PNG download started.",
                                    "success"
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "conversion_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool: "jpg_to_png",
                                        output_type: "png"
                                    }
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "download",
                                    {
                                        tool: "jpg_to_png",
                                        file_type: "png"
                                    }
                                );

                                setTimeout(
                                    function () {
                                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                            downloadUrl
                                        );
                                    },
                                    5000
                                );

                                convertButton.disabled =
                                    false;

                            },
                            "image/png"
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        console.error(error);

                        setStatus(
                            "Conversion failed. Please try another JPG image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "conversion_error",
                            {
                                tool: "jpg_to_png",
                                reason: "conversion_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        convertButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the JPG image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "conversion_error",
                        {
                            tool: "jpg_to_png",
                            reason: "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    convertButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const qualityControl =
        document.getElementById("qualityControl");

    const quality =
        document.getElementById("quality");

    const qualityValue =
        document.getElementById("qualityValue");

    const convertButton =
        document.getElementById("convertButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    let selectedFile = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {
        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(message, type = "") {
        status.textContent = message;
        status.className = "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(type);
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {
        selectedFile = null;

        fileInfo.style.display = "none";

        qualityControl.style.display = "none";

        convertButton.style.display = "none";
        convertButton.disabled = true;

        setStatus("");
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        const isJpg =
            file.type === "image/jpeg" ||
            /\.(jpg|jpeg)$/i.test(file.name);

        if (!isJpg) {

            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a JPG or JPEG image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag("event", "conversion_error", {
                tool: "jpg_to_webp",
                reason: "invalid_file_type"
            });

            return;
        }

        selectedFile = file;

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(file.size);

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "block";

        convertButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        convertButton.disabled =
            false;

        setStatus(
            "Image ready to convert."
        );

        gtag("event", "file_selected", {
            tool: "jpg_to_webp",
            file_type: "jpg"
        });
    }

    quality.addEventListener(
        "input",
        function () {
            qualityValue.textContent =
                this.value + "%";
        }
    );

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {
            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {
            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            const file =
                event.dataTransfer.files[0];

            handleFile(file);
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    convertButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select a JPG image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            convertButton.disabled =
                true;

            setStatus(
                "Converting..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "conversion_started",
                {
                    tool: "jpg_to_webp",
                    quality: Number(
                        quality.value
                    )
                }
            );

            const image =
                new Image();

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        if (
                            image.naturalWidth === 0 ||
                            image.naturalHeight === 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid image dimensions."
                            );
                        }

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            image.naturalWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            image.naturalHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        const webpQuality =
                            Number(
                                quality.value
                            ) / 100;

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                if (!blob) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Could not create the WebP file.",
                                        "error"
                                    );

                                    gtag(
                                        "event",
                                        "conversion_error",
                                        {
                                            tool: "jpg_to_webp",
                                            reason: "blob_creation_failed"
                                        }
                                    );

                                    convertButton.disabled =
                                        false;

                                    return;
                                }

                                const downloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                const link =
                                    document.createElement(
                                        "a"
                                    );

                                const outputName =
                                    selectedFile.name
                                        .replace(
                                            /\.(jpg|jpeg)$/i,
                                            ""
                                        ) + ".webp";

                                link.href =
                                    downloadUrl;

                                link.download =
                                    outputName;

                                link.style.display =
                                    "none";

                                document.body.appendChild(
                                    link
                                );

                                link.click();

                                document.body.removeChild(
                                    link
                                );

                                setStatus(
                                    "Conversion complete. WebP download started.",
                                    "success"
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "conversion_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool: "jpg_to_webp",
                                        output_type: "webp",
                                        quality: Number(
                                            quality.value
                                        )
                                    }
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "download",
                                    {
                                        tool: "jpg_to_webp",
                                        file_type: "webp"
                                    }
                                );

                                setTimeout(
                                    function () {
                                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                            downloadUrl
                                        );
                                    },
                                    5000
                                );

                                convertButton.disabled =
                                    false;

                            },
                            "image/webp",
                            webpQuality
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        console.error(error);

                        setStatus(
                            "Conversion failed. Please try another JPG image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "conversion_error",
                            {
                                tool: "jpg_to_webp",
                                reason: "conversion_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        convertButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the JPG image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "conversion_error",
                        {
                            tool: "jpg_to_webp",
                            reason: "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    convertButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

<!-- PDF.js -->
<script type="module">

    import {
        getDocument,
        GlobalWorkerOptions
    } from "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/pdfjs-dist@6.3.289/build/pdf.mjs";

    GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc =
        "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/pdfjs-dist@6.3.289/build/pdf.worker.mjs";

    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const pageCount =
        document.getElementById("pageCount");

    const settings =
        document.getElementById("settings");

    const quality =
        document.getElementById("quality");

    const scale =
        document.getElementById("scale");

    const convertButton =
        document.getElementById("convertButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    const results =
        document.getElementById("results");

    const resultsCount =
        document.getElementById("resultsCount");

    const pageGrid =
        document.getElementById("pageGrid");

    let selectedFile = null;
    let pdfDocument = null;

    const pageUrls = [];

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {

            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function cleanupPageUrls() {

        pageUrls.forEach(
            function (url) {
                URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            }
        );

        pageUrls.length =
            0;
    }

    function resetResults() {

        cleanupPageUrls();

        pageGrid.innerHTML =
            "";

        results.style.display =
            "none";

        resultsCount.textContent =
            "â€”";
    }

    function isPdf(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return false;
        }

        return (
            file.type === "application/pdf" ||
            /\.pdf$/i.test(
                file.name
            )
        );
    }

    async function loadPdf(file) {

        const bytes =
            await file.arrayBuffer();

        return getDocument({
            data: bytes
        }).promise;
    }

    async function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        if (!isPdf(file)) {

            selectedFile =
                null;

            pdfDocument =
                null;

            fileInfo.style.display =
                "none";

            settings.style.display =
                "none";

            convertButton.disabled =
                true;

            convertButton.style.display =
                "none";

            resetResults();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a PDF file.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "pdf_to_jpg_error",
                {
                    tool:
                        "pdf_to_jpg",
                    reason:
                        "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        resetResults();

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        settings.style.display =
            "block";

        convertButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        convertButton.disabled =
            true;

        setStatus(
            "Loading PDF..."
        );

        try {

            pdfDocument =
                await loadPdf(
                    file
                );

            pageCount.textContent =
                pdfDocument.numPages +
                (
                    pdfDocument.numPages === 1
                        ? " page"
                        : " pages"
                );

            convertButton.disabled =
                false;

            setStatus(
                "PDF ready to convert."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "pdf_file_selected",
                {
                    tool:
                        "pdf_to_jpg",
                    page_count:
                        pdfDocument.numPages
                }
            );

        } catch (error) {

            console.error(error);

            pdfDocument =
                null;

            convertButton.disabled =
                true;

            setStatus(
                "Unable to read this PDF. The file may be damaged or unsupported.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "pdf_to_jpg_error",
                {
                    tool:
                        "pdf_to_jpg",
                    reason:
                        "pdf_load_failed"
                }
            );
        }
    }

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );

            this.value =
                "";
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFile(
                event.dataTransfer.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    async function convertPdf() {

        if (
            !pdfDocument ||
            !selectedFile
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const qualityValue =
            Number(
                quality.value
            );

        const scaleValue =
            Number(
                scale.value
            );

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(
                qualityValue
            ) ||
            qualityValue < 50 ||
            qualityValue > 100
        ) {

            setStatus(
                "JPG quality must be between 50 and 100.",
                "error"
            );

            return;
        }

        convertButton.disabled =
            true;

        resetResults();

        setStatus(
            "Rendering PDF pages..."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "pdf_to_jpg_started",
            {
                tool:
                    "pdf_to_jpg",
                page_count:
                    pdfDocument.numPages,
                quality:
                    qualityValue,
                scale:
                    scaleValue
            }
        );

        try {

            for (
                let pageNumber = 1;
                pageNumber <=
                pdfDocument.numPages;
                pageNumber++
            ) {

                setStatus(
                    "Rendering page " +
                    pageNumber +
                    " of " +
                    pdfDocument.numPages +
                    "..."
                );

                const page =
                    await pdfDocument.getPage(
                        pageNumber
                    );

                const viewport =
                    page.getViewport({
                        scale:
                            scaleValue
                    });

                const canvas =
                    document.createElement(
                        "canvas"
                    );

                const context =
                    canvas.getContext(
                        "2d"
                    );

                if (!context) {

                    throw new Error(
                        "Canvas is not supported."
                    );
                }

                canvas.width =
                    Math.ceil(
                        viewport.width
                    );

                canvas.height =
                    Math.ceil(
                        viewport.height
                    );

                await page.render({
                    canvasContext:
                        context,
                    viewport:
                        viewport
                }).promise;

                const blob =
                    await new Promise(
                        function (
                            resolve
                        ) {

                            canvas.toBlob(
                                resolve,
                                "image/jpeg",
                                qualityValue / 100
                            );
                        }
                    );

                if (!blob) {

                    throw new Error(
                        "Could not create JPG."
                    );
                }

                const objectUrl =
                    URL.createObjectURL(
                        blob
                    );

                pageUrls.push(
                    objectUrl
                );

                const card =
                    document.createElement(
                        "article"
                    );

                card.className =
                    "page-card";

                const cardHeader =
                    document.createElement(
                        "div"
                    );

                cardHeader.className =
                    "page-card-header";

                const number =
                    document.createElement(
                        "span"
                    );

                number.className =
                    "page-number";

                number.textContent =
                    "Page " +
                    pageNumber;

                const dimensions =
                    document.createElement(
                        "span"
                    );

                dimensions.className =
                    "page-size";

                dimensions.textContent =
                    canvas.width +
                    " Ã— " +
                    canvas.height +
                    " px";

                cardHeader.appendChild(
                    number
                );

                cardHeader.appendChild(
                    dimensions
                );

                const preview =
                    document.createElement(
                        "img"
                    );

                preview.className =
                    "page-preview";

                preview.src =
                    objectUrl;

                preview.alt =
                    "Page " +
                    pageNumber +
                    " converted from PDF to JPG";

                const download =
                    document.createElement(
                        "a"
                    );

                download.className =
                    "button page-download";

                download.href =
                    objectUrl;

                download.download =
                    (
                        selectedFile.name
                            .replace(
                                /\.pdf$/i,
                                ""
                            )
                    ) +
                    "-page-" +
                    pageNumber +
                    ".jpg";

                download.textContent =
                    "Download Page " +
                    pageNumber +
                    " as JPG";

                download.addEventListener(
                    "click",
                    function () {

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "download",
                            {
                                tool:
                                    "pdf_to_jpg",
                                file_type:
                                    "jpg",
                                page:
                                    pageNumber
                            }
                        );
                    }
                );

                card.appendChild(
                    cardHeader
                );

                card.appendChild(
                    preview
                );

                card.appendChild(
                    download
                );

                pageGrid.appendChild(
                    card
                );

                results.style.display =
                    "block";

                resultsCount.textContent =
                    pageNumber +
                    " of " +
                    pdfDocument.numPages;
            }

            setStatus(
                "All PDF pages converted successfully.",
                "success"
            );

            resultsCount.textContent =
                pdfDocument.numPages +
                (
                    pdfDocument.numPages === 1
                        ? " page"
                        : " pages"
                );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "pdf_to_jpg_completed",
                {
                    tool:
                        "pdf_to_jpg",
                    page_count:
                        pdfDocument.numPages,
                    quality:
                        qualityValue,
                    scale:
                        scaleValue
                }
            );

        } catch (error) {

            console.error(error);

            setStatus(
                "Conversion failed. Please try another PDF.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "pdf_to_jpg_error",
                {
                    tool:
                        "pdf_to_jpg",
                    reason:
                        "render_failed"
                }
            );

        } finally {

            convertButton.disabled =
                false;
        }
    }

    convertButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        convertPdf
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupPageUrls();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

    <section class="content-section">

        <h2>
            What to know about PNG compression
        </h2>

        <p>
            PNG uses lossless compression, so it is well suited
            to graphics, screenshots and images where preserving
            exact pixel information matters.
        </p>

        <p>
            PNG file size depends heavily on the image contents
            and how the file was originally encoded. Because of
            that, reprocessing a PNG does not guarantee a smaller
            file.
        </p>

        <p>
            Kyvorix shows the actual resulting size instead of
            claiming a fixed percentage reduction.
        </p>

        <div class="tips">

            <div class="tip">

                <strong>
                    Transparent images
                </strong>

                <span>
                    PNG supports transparency, and this tool does
                    not intentionally replace transparent pixels
                    with a background.
                </span>

            </div>

            <div class="tip">

                <strong>
                    Already optimized files
                </strong>

                <span>
                    A PNG that has already been optimized may
                    show little or no size reduction.
                </span>

            </div>

            <div class="tip">

                <strong>
                    Screenshots
                </strong>

                <span>
                    PNG is often useful for screenshots and
                    graphics containing sharp edges or text.
                </span>

            </div>

            <div class="tip">

                <strong>
                    Check the actual result
                </strong>

                <span>
                    Compare the original and optimized sizes
                    before deciding which file to keep.
                </span>

            </div>

        </div>

    </section>

    <section class="content-section">

        <div class="faq">

            <details>

                <summary>

                <p>
                    Yes. Kyvorix provides a free PNG compressor
                    that works directly in your browser.

            <details>

                <summary>
                    Are my PNG files uploaded?

                <p>
                    No. PNG processing is performed directly
                    in your browser, so the image does not need
                    to be uploaded to a Kyvorix server.
                </p>

            </details>

            <details>

                <summary>
                    Will every PNG become smaller?
                </summary>

                <p>
                    No. Reprocessing may produce a smaller,
                    similar or larger file depending on the
                    original PNG.
                </p>

            </details>

            <details>

                <summary>
                    Does PNG compression remove transparency?
                </summary>

                <p>
                    No. The output remains a PNG and transparent
                    pixels are not intentionally replaced with
                    a solid background.
                </p>

            </details>

        </div>

<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const compressButton =
        document.getElementById("compressButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    const result =
        document.getElementById("result");

    const originalSize =
        document.getElementById("originalSize");

    const optimizedSize =
        document.getElementById("optimizedSize");

    const savedPercent =
        document.getElementById("savedPercent");

    const downloadButton =
        document.getElementById("downloadButton");

    let selectedFile = null;
    let optimizedBlob = null;
    let optimizedDownloadUrl = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function cleanupDownloadUrl() {

        if (optimizedDownloadUrl) {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
                optimizedDownloadUrl
            );

            optimizedDownloadUrl =
                null;
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {

        selectedFile = null;
        optimizedBlob = null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "none";

        compressButton.style.display =
            "none";

        compressButton.disabled =
            true;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus("");
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        const isPng =
            file.type === "image/png" ||
            /\.png$/i.test(
                file.name
            );

        if (!isPng) {

            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a PNG image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "png_compression_error",
                {
                    tool:
                        "png_compressor",
                    reason:
                        "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        optimizedBlob =
            null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        compressButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        compressButton.disabled =
            false;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus(
            "PNG ready to optimize."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "file_selected",
            {
                tool:
                    "png_compressor",
                file_type:
                    "png"
            }
        );
    }

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFile(
                event.dataTransfer.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    compressButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select a PNG image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            compressButton.disabled =
                true;

            result.style.display =
                "none";

            downloadButton.style.display =
                "none";

            setStatus(
                "Optimizing PNG..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "compression_started",
                {
                    tool:
                        "png_compressor"
                }
            );

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            const image =
                new Image();

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        if (
                            image.naturalWidth === 0 ||
                            image.naturalHeight === 0
                        ) {

                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid image dimensions."
                            );
                        }

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            image.naturalWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            image.naturalHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {

                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        /*
                         * Do not paint a background.
                         * PNG transparency is preserved.
                         */
                        context.clearRect(
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                    sourceUrl
                                );

                                if (!blob) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Could not create the optimized PNG.",
                                        "error"
                                    );

                                    gtag(
                                        "event",
                                        "png_compression_error",
                                        {
                                            tool:
                                                "png_compressor",
                                            reason:
                                                "blob_creation_failed"
                                        }
                                    );

                                    compressButton.disabled =
                                        false;

                                    return;
                                }

                                optimizedBlob =
                                    blob;

                                cleanupDownloadUrl();

                                optimizedDownloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                const originalBytes =
                                    selectedFile.size;

                                const optimizedBytes =
                                    blob.size;

                                const saved =
                                    originalBytes > 0
                                        ? (
                                            (
                                                originalBytes -
                                                optimizedBytes
                                            ) /
                                            originalBytes
                                        ) * 100
                                        : 0;

                                originalSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        originalBytes
                                    );

                                optimizedSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        optimizedBytes
                                    );

                                savedPercent.textContent =
                                    (
                                        Math.max(
                                            0,
                                            saved
                                        )
                                    ).toFixed(
                                        1
                                    ) + "%";

                                result.style.display =
                                    "block";

                                /*
                                 * There is no guarantee that
                                 * browser PNG re-encoding will
                                 * reduce the file size.
                                 */
                                if (
                                    optimizedBytes <
                                    originalBytes
                                ) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "PNG optimization complete.",
                                        "success"
                                    );

                                } else if (
                                    optimizedBytes ===
                                    originalBytes
                                ) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "The optimized PNG is the same size as the original.",
                                        "warning"
                                    );

                                } else {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "The optimized PNG is larger than the original. Keeping the original is recommended.",
                                        "warning"
                                    );
                                }

                                downloadButton.style.display =
                                    "inline-flex";

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "compression_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool:
                                            "png_compressor",
                                        input_type:
                                            "png",
                                        output_type:
                                            "png",
                                        original_bytes:
                                            originalBytes,
                                        optimized_bytes:
                                            optimizedBytes,
                                        saved_percent:
                                            Number(
                                                Math.max(
                                                    0,
                                                    saved
                                                ).toFixed(
                                                    1
                                                )
                                            )
                                    }
                                );

                                compressButton.disabled =
                                    false;
                            },
                            "image/png"
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            sourceUrl
                        );

                        console.error(error);

                        setStatus(
                            "PNG optimization failed. Please try another image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "png_compression_error",
                            {
                                tool:
                                    "png_compressor",
                                reason:
                                    "compression_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        compressButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the PNG image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "png_compression_error",
                        {
                            tool:
                                "png_compressor",
                            reason:
                                "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    compressButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );

    downloadButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !optimizedBlob ||
                !optimizedDownloadUrl ||
                !selectedFile
            ) {
                return;
            }

            const outputName =
                selectedFile.name
                    .replace(
                        /\.png$/i,
                        ""
                    ) +
                "-optimized.png";

            const link =
                document.createElement(
                    "a"
                );

            link.href =
                optimizedDownloadUrl;

            link.download =
                outputName;

            link.style.display =
                "none";

            document.body.appendChild(
                link
            );

            link.click();

            document.body.removeChild(
                link
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "download",
                {
                    tool:
                        "png_compressor",
                    file_type:
                        "png"
                }
            );
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupDownloadUrl();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

    <!-- FAQ structured data -->

    <section class="content-section">

        <h2>
            Why convert PNG to JPG?
        </h2>

        <p>
            JPG is commonly used for photographs and web images
            because JPEG compression can produce substantially
            smaller files than uncompressed or lossless formats
            in many situations.
        </p>

        <p>
            Converting a PNG to JPG can therefore be useful when
            you need a smaller image file or when a service,
            website, or application specifically requires JPG.
        </p>

        <p>
            Keep in mind that JPG uses lossy compression.
            Converting a PNG to JPG can reduce image quality,
            especially at lower quality settings.
        </p>

    </section>

    <section class="content-section">

        <h2>

                <summary>

                </summary>

<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const qualityControl =
        document.getElementById("qualityControl");

    const quality =
        document.getElementById("quality");

    const qualityValue =
        document.getElementById("qualityValue");

    const convertButton =
        document.getElementById("convertButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    let selectedFile = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {
        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(message, type = "") {
        status.textContent = message;
        status.className = "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(type);
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {
        selectedFile = null;

        fileInfo.style.display = "none";

        qualityControl.style.display = "none";

        convertButton.style.display = "none";
        convertButton.disabled = true;

        setStatus("");
    }

    function handleFile(file) {
        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        const isPng =
            file.type === "image/png" ||
            /\.png$/i.test(file.name);

        if (!isPng) {
            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a PNG image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag("event", "conversion_error", {
                tool: "png_to_jpg",
                reason: "invalid_file_type"
            });

            return;
        }

        selectedFile = file;

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(file.size);

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "block";

        convertButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        convertButton.disabled =
            false;

        setStatus(
            "Image ready to convert."
        );

        gtag("event", "file_selected", {
            tool: "png_to_jpg",
            file_type: "png"
        });
    }

    quality.addEventListener(
        "input",
        function () {
            qualityValue.textContent =
                this.value + "%";
        }
    );

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {
            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {
            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            const file =
                event.dataTransfer.files[0];

            handleFile(file);
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {
            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {
                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    convertButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select a PNG image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            convertButton.disabled =
                true;

            setStatus(
                "Converting..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "conversion_started",
                {
                    tool: "png_to_jpg"
                }
            );

            const image =
                new Image();

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        if (
                            image.naturalWidth === 0 ||
                            image.naturalHeight === 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid image dimensions."
                            );
                        }

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            image.naturalWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            image.naturalHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        /*
                         * JPG does not support transparency.
                         * Paint a white background first.
                         */
                        context.fillStyle =
                            "#ffffff";

                        context.fillRect(
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        const jpegQuality =
                            Number(quality.value) / 100;

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                if (!blob) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Could not create the JPG file.",
                                        "error"
                                    );

                                    gtag(
                                        "event",
                                        "conversion_error",
                                        {
                                            tool: "png_to_jpg",
                                            reason: "blob_creation_failed"
                                        }
                                    );

                                    convertButton.disabled =
                                        false;

                                    return;
                                }

                                const downloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                const link =
                                    document.createElement(
                                        "a"
                                    );

                                const outputName =
                                    selectedFile.name
                                        .replace(
                                            /\.png$/i,
                                            ""
                                        ) + ".jpg";

                                link.href =
                                    downloadUrl;

                                link.download =
                                    outputName;

                                link.style.display =
                                    "none";

                                document.body.appendChild(
                                    link
                                );

                                link.click();

                                document.body.removeChild(
                                    link
                                );

                                setStatus(
                                    "Conversion complete. JPG download started.",
                                    "success"
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "conversion_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool: "png_to_jpg",
                                        output_type: "jpg",
                                        quality: Number(
                                            quality.value
                                        )
                                    }
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "download",
                                    {
                                        tool: "png_to_jpg",
                                        file_type: "jpg"
                                    }
                                );

                                setTimeout(
                                    function () {
                                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                            downloadUrl
                                        );
                                    },
                                    5000
                                );

                                convertButton.disabled =
                                    false;

                            },
                            "image/jpeg",
                            jpegQuality
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        console.error(error);

                        setStatus(
                            "Conversion failed. Please try another PNG image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "conversion_error",
                            {
                                tool: "png_to_jpg",
                                reason: "conversion_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        convertButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the PNG image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "conversion_error",
                        {
                            tool: "png_to_jpg",
                            reason: "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    convertButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

        <h2>
            Why convert PNG to WebP?
        </h2>

        <p>
            WebP is designed for efficient image delivery on
            the web and can produce smaller files than PNG in
            many situations.
        </p>

        <p>
            Converting PNG images to WebP can therefore be useful
            when preparing graphics and other images for websites
            where file size and loading performance matter.
        </p>

        <p>
            WebP also supports transparency, making it useful
            for many images that would otherwise be stored as PNG.
        </p>
