    const result =
        document.getElementById("result");

    const originalSize =
        document.getElementById("originalSize");

    const webpSize =
        document.getElementById("webpSize");

    const savedPercent =
        document.getElementById("savedPercent");

    const downloadButton =
        document.getElementById("downloadButton");

    let selectedFile = null;
    let convertedBlob = null;
    let convertedDownloadUrl = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {

            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {

            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function cleanupDownloadUrl() {

        if (convertedDownloadUrl) {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
                convertedDownloadUrl
            );

            convertedDownloadUrl =
                null;
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {

        selectedFile = null;

        convertedBlob = null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "none";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "none";

        convertButton.style.display =
            "none";

        convertButton.disabled =
            true;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus("");
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        const isPng =
            file.type === "image/png" ||
            /\.png$/i.test(file.name);

        if (!isPng) {

            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a PNG image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "conversion_error",
                {
                    tool: "png_to_webp",
                    reason:
                        "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        convertedBlob =
            null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "block";

        convertButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        convertButton.disabled =
            false;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus(
            "Image ready to convert."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "file_selected",
            {
                tool:
                    "png_to_webp",
                file_type:
                    "png"
            }
        );
    }

    quality.addEventListener(
        "input",
        function () {

            qualityValue.textContent =
                this.value + "%";
        }
    );

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFile(
                event.dataTransfer.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    convertButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select a PNG image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            convertButton.disabled =
                true;

            result.style.display =
                "none";

            downloadButton.style.display =
                "none";

            setStatus(
                "Converting..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "conversion_started",
                {
                    tool:
                        "png_to_webp",
                    quality:
                        Number(
                            quality.value
                        )
                }
            );

            const image =
                new Image();

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        if (
                            image.naturalWidth === 0 ||
                            image.naturalHeight === 0
                        ) {

                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid image dimensions."
                            );
                        }

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            image.naturalWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            image.naturalHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {

                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        /*
                         * Do not paint a background.
                         * WebP supports transparency.
                         */
                        context.clearRect(
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        const webpQuality =
                            Number(
                                quality.value
                            ) / 100;

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                    sourceUrl
                                );

                                if (!blob) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Could not create the WebP file.",
                                        "error"
                                    );

                                    gtag(
                                        "event",
                                        "conversion_error",
                                        {
                                            tool:
                                                "png_to_webp",
                                            reason:
                                                "blob_creation_failed"
                                        }
                                    );

                                    convertButton.disabled =
                                        false;

                                    return;
                                }

                                convertedBlob =
                                    blob;

                                cleanupDownloadUrl();

                                convertedDownloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                const originalBytes =
                                    selectedFile.size;

                                const convertedBytes =
                                    blob.size;

                                const saved =
                                    originalBytes > 0
                                        ? Math.max(
                                            0,
                                            (
                                                (
                                                    originalBytes -
                                                    convertedBytes
                                                ) /
                                                originalBytes
                                            ) * 100
                                        )
                                        : 0;

                                originalSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        originalBytes
                                    );

                                webpSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        convertedBytes
                                    );

                                if (
                                    convertedBytes <
                                    originalBytes
                                ) {

                                    savedPercent.textContent =
                                        saved.toFixed(
                                            1
                                        ) + "%";

                                } else {

                                    savedPercent.textContent =
                                        "0%";
                                }

                                result.style.display =
                                    "block";

                                downloadButton.style.display =
                                    "inline-flex";

                                setStatus(
                                    "Conversion complete. Your WebP is ready to download.",
                                    "success"
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "conversion_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool:
                                            "png_to_webp",
                                        output_type:
                                            "webp",
                                        quality:
                                            Number(
                                                quality.value
                                            ),
                                        original_bytes:
                                            originalBytes,
                                        output_bytes:
                                            convertedBytes,
                                        saved_percent:
                                            Number(
                                                saved.toFixed(
                                                    1
                                                )
                                            )
                                    }
                                );

                                convertButton.disabled =
                                    false;
                            },
                            "image/webp",
                            webpQuality
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            sourceUrl
                        );

                        console.error(error);

                        setStatus(
                            "Conversion failed. Please try another PNG image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "conversion_error",
                            {
                                tool:
                                    "png_to_webp",
                                reason:
                                    "conversion_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        convertButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the PNG image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "conversion_error",
                        {
                            tool:
                                "png_to_webp",
                            reason:
                                "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    convertButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );

    downloadButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !convertedBlob ||
                !selectedFile ||
                !convertedDownloadUrl
            ) {
                return;
            }

            const outputName =
                selectedFile.name
                    .replace(
                        /\.png$/i,
                        ""
                    ) +
                ".webp";

            const link =
                document.createElement(
                    "a"
                );

            link.href =
                convertedDownloadUrl;

            link.download =
                outputName;

            link.style.display =
                "none";

            document.body.appendChild(
                link
            );

            link.click();

            document.body.removeChild(
                link
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "download",
                {
                    tool:
                        "png_to_webp",
                    file_type:
                        "webp"
                }
            );
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupDownloadUrl();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const qualityControl =
        document.getElementById("qualityControl");

    const quality =
        document.getElementById("quality");

    const qualityValue =
        document.getElementById("qualityValue");

    const compressButton =
        document.getElementById("compressButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    const result =
        document.getElementById("result");

    const originalSize =
        document.getElementById("originalSize");

    const compressedSize =
        document.getElementById("compressedSize");

    const savedPercent =
        document.getElementById("savedPercent");

    const downloadButton =
        document.getElementById("downloadButton");

    let selectedFile = null;
    let compressedBlob = null;
    let compressedDownloadUrl = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function cleanupDownloadUrl() {

        if (compressedDownloadUrl) {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(
                compressedDownloadUrl
            );

            compressedDownloadUrl =
                null;
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {

        selectedFile = null;
        compressedBlob = null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "none";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "none";

        compressButton.style.display =
            "none";

        compressButton.disabled =
            true;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus("");
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        const isWebP =
            file.type === "image/webp" ||
            /\.webp$/i.test(
                file.name
            );

        if (!isWebP) {

            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a WebP image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "webp_compression_error",
                {
                    tool:
                        "webp_compressor",
                    reason:
                        "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        compressedBlob =
            null;

        cleanupDownloadUrl();

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        qualityControl.style.display =
            "block";

        compressButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        compressButton.disabled =
            false;

        result.style.display =
            "none";

        downloadButton.style.display =
            "none";

        setStatus(
            "WebP ready to compress."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "file_selected",
            {
                tool:
                    "webp_compressor",
                file_type:
                    "webp"
            }
        );
    }

    quality.addEventListener(
        "input",
        function () {

            qualityValue.textContent =
                this.value + "%";
        }
    );

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFile(
                event.dataTransfer.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    compressButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select a WebP image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            compressButton.disabled =
                true;

            result.style.display =
                "none";

            downloadButton.style.display =
                "none";

            setStatus(
                "Compressing WebP..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "compression_started",
                {
                    tool:
                        "webp_compressor",
                    quality:
                        Number(
                            quality.value
                        )
                }
            );

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            const image =
                new Image();

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        if (
                            image.naturalWidth === 0 ||
                            image.naturalHeight === 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid image dimensions."
                            );
                        }

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            image.naturalWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            image.naturalHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        /*
                         * Do not paint a background.
                         * WebP can preserve transparency.
                         */
                        context.clearRect(
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        const webpQuality =
                            Number(
                                quality.value
                            ) / 100;

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                    sourceUrl
                                );

                                if (!blob) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Could not create the compressed WebP.",
                                        "error"
                                    );

                                    gtag(
                                        "event",
                                        "webp_compression_error",
                                        {
                                            tool:
                                                "webp_compressor",
                                            reason:
                                                "blob_creation_failed"
                                        }
                                    );

                                    compressButton.disabled =
                                        false;

                                    return;
                                }

                                compressedBlob =
                                    blob;

                                cleanupDownloadUrl();

                                compressedDownloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                const originalBytes =
                                    selectedFile.size;

                                const compressedBytes =
                                    blob.size;

                                const saved =
                                    originalBytes > 0
                                        ? (
                                            (
                                                originalBytes -
                                                compressedBytes
                                            ) /
                                            originalBytes
                                        ) * 100
                                        : 0;

                                originalSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        originalBytes
                                    );

                                compressedSize.textContent =
                                    formatFileSize(
                                        compressedBytes
                                    );

                                savedPercent.textContent =
                                    Math.max(
                                        0,
                                        saved
                                    ).toFixed(
                                        1
                                    ) + "%";

                                result.style.display =
                                    "block";

                                downloadButton.style.display =
                                    "inline-flex";

                                if (
                                    compressedBytes <
                                    originalBytes
                                ) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Compression complete.",
                                        "success"
                                    );

                                } else if (
                                    compressedBytes ===
                                    originalBytes
                                ) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "The compressed file is the same size as the original.",
                                        "warning"
                                    );

                                } else {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "The compressed file is larger than the original. Try a lower quality setting.",
                                        "warning"
                                    );
                                }

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "compression_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool:
                                            "webp_compressor",
                                        input_type:
                                            "webp",
                                        output_type:
                                            "webp",
                                        quality:
                                            Number(
                                                quality.value
                                            ),
                                        original_bytes:
                                            originalBytes,
                                        compressed_bytes:
                                            compressedBytes,
                                        saved_percent:
                                            Number(
                                                Math.max(
                                                    0,
                                                    saved
                                                ).toFixed(
                                                    1
                                                )
                                            )
                                    }
                                );

                                compressButton.disabled =
                                    false;
                            },
                            "image/webp",
                            webpQuality
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            sourceUrl
                        );

                        console.error(error);

                        setStatus(
                            "Compression failed. Please try another WebP image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "webp_compression_error",
                            {
                                tool:
                                    "webp_compressor",
                                reason:
                                    "compression_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        compressButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the WebP image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "webp_compression_error",
                        {
                            tool:
                                "webp_compressor",
                            reason:
                                "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    compressButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );

    downloadButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                !compressedBlob ||
                !compressedDownloadUrl ||
                !selectedFile
            ) {
                return;
            }

            const outputName =
                selectedFile.name
                    .replace(
                        /\.webp$/i,
                        ""
                    ) +
                "-compressed.webp";

            const link =
                document.createElement(
                    "a"
                );

            link.href =
                compressedDownloadUrl;

            link.download =
                outputName;

            link.style.display =
                "none";

            document.body.appendChild(
                link
            );

            link.click();

            document.body.removeChild(
                link
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "download",
                {
                    tool:
                        "webp_compressor",
                    file_type:
                        "webp"
                }
            );
        }
    );

    window.addEventListener(
        "beforeunload",
        function () {
            cleanupDownloadUrl();
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>

const fileInput = document.getElementById("fileInput");
const dropzone = document.getElementById("dropzone");
const quality = document.getElementById("quality");
const qualityValue = document.getElementById("qualityValue");
const convertButton = document.getElementById("convertButton");
const message = document.getElementById("message");

const result = document.getElementById("result");
const originalSize = document.getElementById("originalSize");
const outputSize = document.getElementById("outputSize");
const savedPercent = document.getElementById("savedPercent");

const previewImage = document.getElementById("previewImage");
const downloadButton = document.getElementById("downloadButton");

let selectedFile = null;
let outputUrl = null;


quality.addEventListener("input", () => {
    qualityValue.textContent = quality.value;
});


function isWebPFile(file) {
    return file &&
        (
            file.type === "image/webp" ||
            /\.webp$/i.test(file.name)
        );
}


function formatBytes(bytes) {

    if (!bytes) {
        return "0 B";
    }

    const units = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"];

    const index = Math.min(
        Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(1024)),
        units.length - 1
    );

    return `${(bytes / Math.pow(1024, index)).toFixed(2)} ${units[index]}`;
}


function calculateChange(original, output) {

    if (!original) {
        return "â€”";
    }

    const percentage =
        ((original - output) / original) * 100;

    if (percentage > 0) {
        return `${percentage.toFixed(1)}% smaller`;
    }

    if (percentage < 0) {
        return `${Math.abs(percentage).toFixed(1)}% larger`;
    }

    return "Same size";
}


function selectFile(file) {

    if (!isWebPFile(file)) {

        selectedFile = null;
        convertButton.disabled = true;

        message.textContent =
            "Please select a valid WebP image.";

        result.classList.remove("show");

        return;
    }

    selectedFile = file;
    convertButton.disabled = false;

    message.textContent =
        `Selected: ${file.name}`;

    result.classList.remove("show");

    if (outputUrl) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(outputUrl);
        outputUrl = null;
    }

    if (typeof gtag === "function") {

        gtag("event", "file_selected", {
            tool_name: "webp-to-jpg",
            file_type: "webp"
        });

    }
}


fileInput.addEventListener("change", () => {

    const file = fileInput.files[0];

    if (file) {
        selectFile(file);
    }

});


["dragenter", "dragover"].forEach((eventName) => {

    dropzone.addEventListener(eventName, (event) => {

        event.preventDefault();
        dropzone.classList.add("dragover");

    });

});


["dragleave", "drop"].forEach((eventName) => {

    dropzone.addEventListener(eventName, (event) => {

        event.preventDefault();
        dropzone.classList.remove("dragover");

    });

});


dropzone.addEventListener("drop", (event) => {

    const file = event.dataTransfer.files[0];

    if (file) {
        selectFile(file);
    }

});


convertButton.addEventListener("click", async () => {

    if (!selectedFile) {
        return;
    }

    convertButton.disabled = true;
    message.textContent = "Converting image...";
    result.classList.remove("show");

    if (typeof gtag === "function") {

        gtag("event", "conversion_started", {
            tool_name: "webp-to-jpg",
            quality: Number(quality.value)
        });

    }

    try {

        const image = new Image();

        const imageUrl =
            URL.createObjectURL(selectedFile);

        image.onload = () => {

            URL.revokeObjectURL(imageUrl);

            const canvas =
                document.createElement("canvas");

            canvas.width = image.naturalWidth;
            canvas.height = image.naturalHeight;

            const context =
                canvas.getContext("2d");

            if (!context) {
                throw new Error("Canvas is not supported.");
            }

            // JPG does not support transparency.
            context.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
            context.fillRect(
                0,
                0,
                canvas.width,
                canvas.height
            );

            context.drawImage(
                image,
                0,
                0
            );


            canvas.toBlob(
                (blob) => {

                    if (!blob) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Could not create JPG."
                        );
                    }

                    if (outputUrl) {
                        URL.revokeObjectURL(outputUrl);
                    }

                    outputUrl =
                        URL.createObjectURL(blob);

                    previewImage.src =
                        outputUrl;

                    const baseName =
                        selectedFile.name
                            .replace(/\.webp$/i, "");

                    downloadButton.href =
                        outputUrl;

                    downloadButton.download =
                        `${baseName}.jpg`;

                    originalSize.textContent =
                        formatBytes(selectedFile.size);

                    outputSize.textContent =
                        formatBytes(blob.size);

                    savedPercent.textContent =
                        calculateChange(
                            selectedFile.size,
                            blob.size
                        );

                    result.classList.add("show");

                    message.textContent =
                        "Conversion complete.";

                    convertButton.disabled = false;

                    if (typeof gtag === "function") {

                        gtag("event", "conversion_completed", {
                            tool_name: "webp-to-jpg",
                            quality: Number(quality.value)
                        });

                    }

                },
                "image/jpeg",
                Number(quality.value) / 100
            );

        };


        image.onerror = () => {

            throw new Error(
                "Could not read the WebP image."
            );

        };

        image.src =
            URL.createObjectURL(selectedFile);

    } catch (error) {

        console.error(error);

        message.textContent =
            "Something went wrong while converting the image.";

        convertButton.disabled = false;

        if (typeof gtag === "function") {

            gtag("event", "conversion_error", {
                tool_name: "webp-to-jpg"
            });

        }

    }

});


downloadButton.addEventListener("click", () => {

    if (typeof gtag === "function") {

        gtag("event", "download", {
            tool_name: "webp-to-jpg"
        });

    }

});

</script>

    <section class="content-section">

        <h2>
            Why convert WebP to PNG?
        </h2>

        <p>
            WebP is designed for efficient web delivery, while
            PNG is widely used for graphics, screenshots,
            editing workflows and images that require
            transparency.
        </p>

        <p>
            Converting WebP to PNG can therefore be useful when
            another application or workflow specifically requires
            a PNG file.
        </p>

        <p>
            PNG uses lossless compression, but converting a lossy
            WebP image to PNG cannot restore image detail that was
            already lost in the original WebP.
        </p>

    </section>

    <section class="content-section">

        <h2>
            WebP vs PNG
        </h2>

        <table class="comparison">

            <thead>
                <tr>
                    <th>Feature</th>
                    <th>WebP</th>
                    <th>PNG</th>
                </tr>

                    <td>Lossy or lossless</td>

                    <td>Supported</td>
                    <td>Supported</td>
                </tr>

                <tr>
                    <td>Typical use</td>
                    <td>Modern web images</td>
                    <td>Graphics and editing</td>
                </tr>

                <tr>
                    <td>Typical file size</td>
                    <td>Often smaller</td>
                    <td>Often larger</td>
                </tr>

            </tbody>

        </table>

        <h2>
            WebP to PNG Converter FAQ
        </h2>

        <div class="faq">

            <details>

                <summary>
                    Can I convert WebP to PNG for free?
                </summary>

                <p>
                    Yes. Kyvorix provides a free WebP to PNG
                    converter that works directly in your browser.
                </p>

            </details>

            <details>

                <summary>
                    Are my WebP files uploaded?
                </summary>

                <p>
                    No. The conversion is performed directly
                    in your browser, so the WebP image does not
                    need to be uploaded to a Kyvorix server.
                </p>

            </details>

            <details>

                <summary>
                    Does WebP to PNG preserve transparency?
                </summary>

                <p>
                    Yes. PNG supports transparency, so transparent
                    areas in a WebP image can be preserved when the
                    image is decoded by the browser.
                </p>

            </details>

            <details>

                <summary>
                    Can I convert an animated WebP?
                </summary>

                <p>
                    This converter processes a WebP as a still image.
                    Animated WebP files are not converted as an
                    animation sequence.
                </p>

            </details>

        </div>

    <div class="footer-inner">

        <a
            href="../index.html"

<script>
    const fileInput =
        document.getElementById("fileInput");

    const dropZone =
        document.getElementById("dropZone");

    const fileInfo =
        document.getElementById("fileInfo");

    const fileName =
        document.getElementById("fileName");

    const fileSize =
        document.getElementById("fileSize");

    const convertButton =
        document.getElementById("convertButton");

    const status =
        document.getElementById("status");

    let selectedFile = null;

    function formatFileSize(bytes) {

        if (bytes < 1024) {
            return bytes + " B";
        }

        if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
            return (
                bytes / 1024
            ).toFixed(1) + " KB";
        }

        return (
            bytes / (1024 * 1024)
        ).toFixed(1) + " MB";
    }

    function setStatus(
        message,
        type = ""
    ) {

        status.textContent =
            message;

        status.className =
            "status";

        if (type) {
            status.classList.add(
                type
            );
        }
    }

    function resetConverter() {

        selectedFile = null;

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "none";

        convertButton.style.display =
            "none";

        convertButton.disabled =
            true;

        setStatus("");
    }

    function handleFile(file) {

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }

        const isWebP =
            file.type === "image/webp" ||
            /\.webp$/i.test(file.name);

        if (!isWebP) {

            resetConverter();

            setStatus(
                "Please select a WebP image.",
                "error"
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "conversion_error",
                {
                    tool: "webp_to_png",
                    reason: "invalid_file_type"
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        selectedFile =
            file;

        fileName.textContent =
            file.name;

        fileSize.textContent =
            formatFileSize(
                file.size
            );

        fileInfo.style.display =
            "block";

        convertButton.style.display =
            "inline-flex";

        convertButton.disabled =
            false;

        setStatus(
            "Image ready to convert."
        );

        gtag(
            "event",
            "file_selected",
            {
                tool: "webp_to_png",
                file_type: "webp"
            }
        );
    }

    fileInput.addEventListener(
        "change",
        function () {

            handleFile(
                this.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragover",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.add(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "dragleave",
        function () {

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "drop",
        function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            dropZone.classList.remove(
                "dragover"
            );

            handleFile(
                event.dataTransfer.files[0]
            );
        }
    );

    dropZone.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        function (event) {

            if (
                event.key === "Enter" ||
                event.key === " "
            ) {

                event.preventDefault();

                fileInput.click();
            }
        }
    );

    convertButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (!selectedFile) {

                setStatus(
                    "Please select a WebP image first.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;
            }

            convertButton.disabled =
                true;

            setStatus(
                "Converting..."
            );

            gtag(
                "event",
                "conversion_started",
                {
                    tool: "webp_to_png"
                }
            );

            const image =
                new Image();

            const sourceUrl =
                URL.createObjectURL(
                    selectedFile
                );

            image.onload =
                function () {

                    try {

                        if (
                            image.naturalWidth === 0 ||
                            image.naturalHeight === 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid image dimensions."
                            );
                        }

                        const canvas =
                            document.createElement(
                                "canvas"
                            );

                        canvas.width =
                            image.naturalWidth;

                        canvas.height =
                            image.naturalHeight;

                        const context =
                            canvas.getContext(
                                "2d"
                            );

                        if (!context) {

                            throw new Error(
                                "Canvas is not supported."
                            );
                        }

                        /*
                         * No background is painted here.
                         * PNG supports transparency.
                         */
                        context.clearRect(
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        context.drawImage(
                            image,
                            0,
                            0,
                            canvas.width,
                            canvas.height
                        );

                        canvas.toBlob(
                            function (blob) {

                                URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                    sourceUrl
                                );

                                if (!blob) {

                                    setStatus(
                                        "Could not create the PNG file.",
                                        "error"
                                    );

                                    gtag(
                                        "event",
                                        "conversion_error",
                                        {
                                            tool:
                                                "webp_to_png",
                                            reason:
                                                "blob_creation_failed"
                                        }
                                    );

                                    convertButton.disabled =
                                        false;

                                    return;
                                }

                                const downloadUrl =
                                    URL.createObjectURL(
                                        blob
                                    );

                                const link =
                                    document.createElement(
                                        "a"
                                    );

                                const outputName =
                                    selectedFile.name
                                        .replace(
                                            /\.webp$/i,
                                            ""
                                        ) +
                                    ".png";

                                link.href =
                                    downloadUrl;

                                link.download =
                                    outputName;

                                link.style.display =
                                    "none";

                                document.body.appendChild(
                                    link
                                );

                                link.click();

                                document.body.removeChild(
                                    link
                                );

                                setStatus(
                                    "Conversion complete. PNG download started.",
                                    "success"
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "conversion_completed",
                                    {
                                        tool:
                                            "webp_to_png",
                                        output_type:
                                            "png"
                                    }
                                );

                                gtag(
                                    "event",
                                    "download",
                                    {
                                        tool:
                                            "webp_to_png",
                                        file_type:
                                            "png"
                                    }
                                );

                                setTimeout(
                                    function () {

                                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                                            downloadUrl
                                        );

                                    },
                                    5000
                                );

                                convertButton.disabled =
                                    false;
                            },
                            "image/png"
                        );

                    } catch (error) {

                        console.error(error);

                        URL.revokeObjectURL(
                            sourceUrl
                        );

                        setStatus(
                            "Conversion failed. Please try another WebP image.",
                            "error"
                        );

                        gtag(
                            "event",
                            "conversion_error",
                            {
                                tool:
                                    "webp_to_png",
                                reason:
                                    "conversion_failed"
                            }
                        );

                        convertButton.disabled =
                            false;
                    }
                };

            image.onerror =
                function () {

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(
                        sourceUrl
                    );

                    setStatus(
                        "Unable to read the WebP image.",
                        "error"
                    );

                    gtag(
                        "event",
                        "conversion_error",
                        {
                            tool:
                                "webp_to_png",
                            reason:
                                "image_load_failed"
                        }
                    );

                    convertButton.disabled =
                        false;
                };

            image.src =
                sourceUrl;
        }
    );
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>

const exploreToolsButton =
    document.getElementById("exploreToolsButton");

if (exploreToolsButton) {

    exploreToolsButton.addEventListener("click", function () {

        if (typeof gtag === "function") {

            gtag("event", "explore_tools", {
                event_category: "navigation",
                event_label: "Explore tools"
            });

        }

    });

}


document
    .querySelectorAll(".tool-card[data-tool]")
    .forEach(function (tool) {

        tool.addEventListener("click", function () {

            const toolName =
                this.getAttribute("data-tool");

            if (typeof gtag === "function") {

                gtag("event", "tool_open", {

                    tool_name: toolName,

                    event_category: "tools",

                    event_label: toolName

                });

            }

        });

    });

</script>

</body>
</html>


============================================================
FILE: C:\Users\PC\Desktop\KYVORIX_TOOLS\script.js
============================================================
"use strict";

// ======================================================
// KYVORIX TOOLS
// Main site script
// ======================================================

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {

    // Smooth navigation for internal links
    document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach((link) => {

        link.addEventListener("click", (event) => {

            const targetId = link.getAttribute("href");

            if (!targetId || targetId === "#") {
                return;
            }

            const target = document.querySelector(targetId);

            if (!target) {
                return;
            }

            event.preventDefault();

            target.scrollIntoView({
                behavior: "smooth",
                block: "start"
            });
        });
    });

});
